\displaystyle\left\lVert\int_{\mathcal{Z}}K_{Z}\mathbf{1}\,d\mu_{T}-\int_{\mathcal{Z}}K_{Z}\mathbf{1}\,d\mu_{S}\right\rVert_{\mathcal{H}_{K_{\ell}}}=\left\lVert\int_{\mathcal{Z}}K_{Z}\mathbf{1}\,d(\mu_{T}-\mu_{S})\right\rVert_{\mathcal{H}_{K_{\ell}}}

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\left\lVert\int_{\mathcal{Z}\times\mathcal{Z}}\left(K_{Z}\mathbf{1}-K_{Z^{\prime}}\mathbf{1}\right)\,d\pi(Z,Z^{\prime})\right\rVert_{\mathcal{H}_{K_{\ell}}}\leq\int_{\mathcal{Z}\times\mathcal{Z}}\left\lVert K_{Z}\mathbf{1}-K_{Z^{\prime}}\mathbf{1}\right\rVert_{\mathcal{H}_{K_{\ell}}}\,d\pi(Z,Z^{\prime})

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\inf_{\pi\in\Pi(\mu_{S},\mu_{T})}\int_{\mathcal{Z}\times\mathcal{Z}}\left\lVert K_{Z}\mathbf{1}-K_{Z^{\prime}}\mathbf{1}\right\rVert_{\mathcal{H}_{K_{\ell}}}\,d\pi(Z,Z^{\prime})=\mathcal{W}_{1}(\mu_{S},\mu_{T}),

(50)

where the used cost function for the Wasserstein 1-distance is

c(Z,Z^{\prime})=\left\lVert K_{Z}\mathbf{1}-K_{Z^{\prime}}\mathbf{1}\right\rVert_{\mathcal{H}_{K_{\ell}}}.

(51)

Substituting Eq. (
50
) into Eq. (
49
), and substituting the term
diff
into Eq. (
E.1
), the result in Eq. (
41
) is obtained.
This completes the proof.
∎

E.2
Bounding Domain Divergence

Below we present and prove the key result on bounding domain divergence, to derive the domain divergence upper bound
\Delta_{D}
.

Lemma E.2

(Wasserstein 2-Domain Divergence)
.

Follow the problem setting explained in the beginning of Section
3
and suppose Assumptions
C.1
,
C.4
and
C.10
are satisfied.
Sample i.i.d source graphs from the source distribution
\mu_{S}
and target graphs from the target distribution
\mu_{T}
to obtain source and target datasets
\mathcal{D}_{S}
and
\mathcal{D}_{T}
, which contain
m_{S}^{j}
source graphs and
m_{T}^{j}
target graphs in each class
j
, with the minimum node numbers
N_{S}
and
N_{T}
.
Define
\hat{P}_{S}^{j}
and
\hat{P}_{T}^{j}
as the resulting empirical estimations of
P_{S}^{j}
and
P_{T}^{j}
through Monte Carlo sampling and
B=\max(1,C_{\mathcal{X}}^{-D_{\mathcal{X}}})
.
Then, the following holds with a probability at least
1-\rho
with
0<\rho<1
:

\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}(\mu_{S},\mu_{T})\leq\Delta_{D},

(52)

where

\displaystyle\Delta_{D}

\displaystyle=2CL_{2}L_{f}^{2}\sum_{j=1}^{C}\bigg[\mathcal{W}_{2}\left(\hat{P}_{S}^{j},\hat{P}_{T}^{j}\right)+B\left(\left(N_{S}\cdot m^{j}_{S}\right)^{-\frac{1}{D_{\mathcal{X}}}}+\left(N_{T}\cdot m^{j}_{T}\right)^{-\frac{1}{D_{\mathcal{X}}}}\right)

\displaystyle+2B\cdot 27^{\frac{D_{\mathcal{X}}}{4}}+B\cdot\log(1/\rho)^{\frac{1}{4}}\left(\left(N_{S}\cdot m^{j}_{S}\right)^{-\frac{1}{4}}+\left(N_{T}\cdot m^{j}_{T}\right)^{-\frac{1}{4}}\right)\bigg]^{2\alpha}.

(53)

Proof.

Applying the result of
\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}\left(\otimes_{i=1}^{n}\mu_{i},\otimes_{i=1}^{n}\nu_{i}\right)=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}(\mu_{i},\nu_{i})
(Chapter 2,
87
), it has

\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}\left(\mu_{S},\mu_{T}\right)=\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}\left(\otimes_{j=1}^{C}f^{j}_{S}\sharp P^{j}_{S},\otimes_{j=1}^{C}f^{j}_{T}\sharp P^{j}_{T}\right)=\sum_{j=1}^{C}\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}\left(f\sharp P^{j}_{S},f\sharp P^{j}_{T}\right).

(54)

We start from analyzing
\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}\left(f\sharp P_{S}^{j},f\sharp P_{T}^{j}\right)
, and temporarily drop the superscription
j
for writing convenience.
To study the push-forward probability measure through the feature function
f:\mathcal{X}\to\mathbb{R}^{F}
, we denote the
\sigma
-Borel algebra on
\mathbb{R}^{F}
by
\mathfrak{B}(\mathbb{R}^{F})
.
Then,
\forall B\in\mathfrak{B}\left(\mathbb{R}^{F}\right)
, it has

\ f_{\sharp}P_{S}(B)=P_{S}\left(f^{-1}(B)\right),\ f_{\sharp}P_{T}(B)=P_{T}\left(f^{-1}(B)\right).

(55)

As a result, for all couplings
\pi\in\Pi(P_{S},P_{T})
, their push-forward couplings
\pi^{\prime}=(f\times f)\sharp\pi
through the function
(f\times f)(x,y)=((f(x),f(y))
satisfies

\displaystyle\pi^{\prime}(A\times\mathbb{R}^{F})

\displaystyle=\pi\left((f\times f)^{-1}(A\times\mathbb{R}^{F})\right)=\pi(f^{-1}(A)\times\mathbb{R}^{D})

\displaystyle=P_{S}(f^{-1}(A))=f_{\sharp}P_{S}(A),

(56)

\displaystyle\pi^{\prime}(\mathbb{R}^{F}\times B)

\displaystyle=\pi\left((f\times f)^{-1}(\mathbb{R}^{F}\times B)\right)=\pi(\mathbb{R}^{D}\times f^{-1}(B))

\displaystyle=P_{T}(f^{-1}(B))=f_{\sharp}P_{T}(B),

(57)

thus,

\pi^{\prime}\in\Pi\left(f_{\sharp}P_{S},f_{\sharp}P_{T}\right),

(58)

for all
A,B\in\mathfrak{B}(\mathbb{R}^{F})
.
Subsequently, it has

\displaystyle\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}(f_{\sharp}P_{S},f_{\sharp}P_{T})

\displaystyle=\inf_{\pi^{\prime}\in\Pi(f_{\sharp}P_{S},f_{\sharp}P_{T})}\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{F}\times\mathbb{R}^{F}}c(f(x),f(y))^{2}d\pi^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\inf_{\pi\in\Pi(P_{S},P_{T})}\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{D}\times\mathbb{R}^{F}}c(f(x),f(y))^{2}\,d\pi.

(59)

Incorporating Eq. (
E.2
) with the superscription
j
restored and the cost function Eq. (
51
) into Eq. (
54
), it has

\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}\left(\mu_{S},\mu_{T}\right)=\sum_{j=1}^{C}\inf_{\pi\in\Pi(P_{S}^{j},P_{T}^{j})}\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{D}\times\mathbb{R}^{F}}\left\lVert K_{Z}\mathbf{1}-K_{Z^{\prime}}\mathbf{1}\right\rVert^{2}_{\mathcal{H}_{K_{\ell}}}\,d\pi(Z,Z^{\prime}).

(60)

Next we focus on analyzing the cost function
\left\lVert K_{Z}\mathbf{1}-K_{Z^{\prime}}\mathbf{1}\right\rVert^{2}_{\mathcal{H}_{K_{\ell}}}
.
It is upper bounded by

\displaystyle\left\lVert K_{Z}\mathbf{1}-K_{Z^{\prime}}\mathbf{1}\right\rVert^{2}_{\mathcal{H}_{K_{\ell}}}

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\langle K_{Z}\mathbf{1}-K_{Z^{\prime}}\mathbf{1},K_{Z}\mathbf{1}-K_{Z^{\prime}}\mathbf{1}\rangle_{\mathcal{H}_{K_{\ell}}}

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\langle K_{Z}\mathbf{1},K_{Z}\mathbf{1}\rangle_{\mathcal{H}_{K_{\ell}}}-2\langle K_{Z}\mathbf{1},K_{Z^{\prime}}\mathbf{1}\rangle_{\mathcal{H}_{K_{\ell}}}+\langle K_{Z^{\prime}}\mathbf{1},K_{Z^{\prime}}\mathbf{1}\rangle_{\mathcal{H}_{K_{\ell}}}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\left|\langle K_{Z}\mathbf{1},K_{Z}\mathbf{1}\rangle_{\mathcal{H}_{K_{\ell}}}-\langle K_{Z}\mathbf{1},K_{Z^{\prime}}\mathbf{1}\rangle_{\mathcal{H}_{K_{\ell}}}\right|+\left|\langle K_{Z}\mathbf{1},K_{Z^{\prime}}\mathbf{1}\rangle_{\mathcal{H}_{K_{\ell}}}-\langle K_{Z^{\prime}}\mathbf{1},K_{Z^{\prime}}\mathbf{1}\rangle_{\mathcal{H}_{K_{\ell}}}\right|.

(61)

Applying the kernel reproducing property in Eq. (
37
) for each item in Eq. (
61
), e.g.,
\langle K_{Z}\mathbf{1},K_{Z}\mathbf{1}\rangle_{\mathcal{H}_{K_{\ell}}}=\langle K(Z,Z)(\mathbf{1}),\mathbf{1}\rangle_{\mathbb{R}^{C}}
and
\langle K_{Z}\mathbf{1},K_{Z^{\prime}}\mathbf{1}\rangle_{\mathcal{H}_{K_{\ell}}}=\langle K(Z,Z^{\prime})(\mathbf{1}),\mathbf{1}\rangle_{\mathbb{R}^{C}}
, it has

\displaystyle\left\lVert K_{Z}\mathbf{1}-K_{Z^{\prime}}\mathbf{1}\right\rVert^{2}_{\mathcal{H}_{K_{\ell}}}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\left|\langle K(Z,Z)(\mathbf{1}),\mathbf{1}\rangle_{\mathbb{R}^{C}}-\langle K(Z,Z^{\prime})(\mathbf{1}),\mathbf{1}\rangle_{\mathbb{R}^{C}}\right|+\left|\langle K(Z,Z^{\prime})(\mathbf{1}),\mathbf{1}\rangle_{\mathbb{R}^{C}}-\langle K(Z^{\prime},Z^{\prime})(\mathbf{1}),\mathbf{1}\rangle_{\mathbb{R}^{C}}\right|

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\left\lvert\sum_{j=1}^{C}\left(K(Z,Z)(\mathbf{1})_{j}-K(Z,Z^{\prime})(\mathbf{1})_{j}\right)\right\rvert+\left\lvert\sum_{j=1}^{C}\left(K(Z,Z^{\prime})(\mathbf{1})_{j}-K(Z^{\prime},Z^{\prime})(\mathbf{1})_{j}\right)\right\rvert

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle C\left\lVert K(Z,Z)(\mathbf{1})-K(Z,Z^{\prime})(\mathbf{1})\right\rVert_{\infty}+C\left\lVert K(Z,Z^{\prime})(\mathbf{1})-K(Z^{\prime},Z^{\prime})(\mathbf{1})\right\rVert_{\infty}.

(62)

Applying Eq. (
36
) in Assumption
C.10
with
\beta=2
and Assumption
C.4
, it has

\displaystyle\left\lVert K_{Z}\mathbf{1}-K_{Z^{\prime}}\mathbf{1}\right\rVert^{2}_{\mathcal{H}_{K_{\ell}}}\leq\;

\displaystyle C(L_{2}(Z)+L_{2}(Z^{\prime}))\cdot d_{\Sigma}(Z,Z^{\prime})^{2}\leq 2CL_{1}\cdot d_{\Sigma}(Z,Z^{\prime})

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle 2CL_{2}\cdot\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\lVert Z_{\sigma(i)}-Z^{\prime}_{i}\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle 2CL_{2}\cdot\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\lVert f\left(x_{\sigma(i)}\right)-f\left(x_{i}^{\prime}\right)\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle 2CL_{2}L_{f}^{2}\cdot\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\lVert x_{\sigma(i)}-x_{i}^{\prime}\rVert^{2\alpha}.

(63)

Define the latent cost function

d_{\max}(Z,Z^{\prime})=\max_{i,j=1}^{N}\lVert x_{i}-x_{j}^{\prime}\rVert,

(64)

where
f(x_{i})=Z_{i}
and
f(x^{\prime}_{i})=Z^{\prime}_{i}
.
Eq. (
E.2
) results in

\left\lVert K_{Z}\mathbf{1}-K_{Z^{\prime}}\mathbf{1}\right\rVert^{2}_{\mathcal{H}_{K_{\ell}}}\leq 2CL_{2}L_{f}^{2}\cdot d_{\max}^{2\alpha}(Z,Z^{\prime}).

(65)

Finally, incorporating Eq. (
65
) into Eq. (
60
), it has

\displaystyle\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}\left(\mu_{S},\mu_{T}\right)\leq\;

\displaystyle 2CL_{2}L_{f}^{2}\sum_{j=1}^{C}\inf_{\pi\in\Pi(P_{S}^{j},P_{T}^{j})}\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{D}\times\mathbb{R}^{F}}d_{\max}^{2\alpha}(Z,Z^{\prime})\,d\pi(Z,Z^{\prime})

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle 2CL_{2}L_{f}^{2}\sum_{j=1}^{C}\left(\inf_{\pi\in\Pi(P_{S}^{j},P_{T}^{j})}\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{D}\times\mathbb{R}^{F}}d_{\max}^{2}(Z,Z^{\prime})\,d\pi(Z,Z^{\prime})\right)^{\alpha}

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle 2CL_{2}L_{f}^{2}\sum_{j=1}^{C}\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2\alpha}\left(P_{S}^{j},P_{T}^{j}\right),

(66)

for which the cost function used for the Wasserstein distance
\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}\left(P_{S}^{j},P_{T}^{j}\right)
is
d_{\max}(Z,Z^{\prime})
.

It’s now left for us to analyze
\mathcal{W}_{2}(P_{S}^{j},P_{T}^{j})
by considering the empirical probability estimations
\hat{P}_{S}^{j}
and
\hat{P}_{T}^{j}
, i.e.,

\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2\alpha}\left(P_{S}^{j},P_{T}^{j}\right)\leq\left(\mathcal{W}_{2}\left(P_{S}^{j},\hat{P}_{S}^{j}\right)+\mathcal{W}_{2}\left(\hat{P}_{S}^{j},\hat{P}_{T}^{j}\right)+\mathcal{W}_{2}\left(P_{T}^{j},\hat{P}_{T}^{j}\right)\right)^{2\alpha}.

(67)

We bound the two quantities
\mathcal{W}_{2}\left(P_{S}^{j},\hat{P}_{S}^{j}\right)
and
\mathcal{W}_{2}\left(P_{T}^{j},\hat{P}_{T}^{j}\right)
by using directly a recent result on empirical Wasserstein distance convergence presented in Theorem
J.5
, i.e.,

\mathcal{W}_{2}\left(P_{\Box}^{j},\hat{P}_{\Box}^{j}\right)\leq B\left({{n_{\Box}^{j}}}^{-\frac{1}{D_{\mathcal{X}}}}+\left(27^{\frac{D_{\mathcal{X}}}{4}}+\log(1/\rho)^{\frac{1}{4}}{{n_{\Box}^{j}}}^{-\frac{1}{4}}\right)\right):=\epsilon_{n_{\Box}}.

(68)

Here and below, we use
\Box
to refer to
S
or
T
depending on the domain.
The sampled dataset
\mathcal{D}_{\Box}=\left\{\left(G_{i}^{j},y_{i}^{j}\right)_{i=1}^{m^{j}_{\Box}}\right\}_{j=1}^{C}
contains
C
classes and
m^{j}_{\Box}
samples for each class
j
.
The number of samples
n^{j}_{\Box}
for estimating
P_{\Box}^{j}
satisfies
n^{j}_{\Box}\geq N_{\Box}\cdot m^{j}_{\Box}
.
Finally, incorporating the expressions of
\epsilon_{n_{\Box}}
and the lower bound of
n^{j}_{\Box}
into Eqs. (
67
) and (
66
), we obtain Eq. (
53
). This completes the proof.

∎

E.3
Bounding Function Norm under Truncated-Spectrum vRKHS

Below we present and prove the key result on bounding loss function complexity in Lem.
E.5
.
Before that, we present Lem.
E.3
, which is a supporting lemma providing results on Lipschitz constant and output perturbation bound by weight changes for an MLP classifier, to be used by Lem
E.5
and Thm.
G.4
.

Lemma E.3

(MLP Lipschitz Constant)
.

Given an
L
-layer MLP classifier
f_{cls}:\mathbb{R}^{F_{T}}\to\mathbb{R}^{C}
as in Def.
B.9
satisfying Assumption
C.8
, denote the Lipschitz constant of the activation function by
L_{\sigma_{l}}
and the maximum eigenvalue of the weight matrix
W_{l}
by
\lambda_{l}
.
The Lipschitz constant of
f_{cls}(x)
with respect to Euclidean norm satisfies

L_{cls}\leq\prod_{l=1}^{L}L_{\sigma_{l}}\lambda_{l}.

(69)

Moreover, applying additive weight changes
\Delta W=\{\Delta W_{l}\}_{l=1}^{L}
, the following holds

\lVert f_{cls}(W+\Delta W;x)-f_{cls}(W;x)\rVert\leq\left(\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\kappa^{2}\right)^{L}\left(\prod_{l=1}^{L}\left(1+\frac{\lVert\Delta W_{l}\rVert_{F}}{\lVert W_{l}\rVert_{F}}\right)-1\right).

(70)

Proof.

Given
x,x^{\prime}\in\mathbb{R}^{F_{T}}
, the linear transformation
W_{l}(x)
results in

\lVert W_{l}(x)-W_{l}(x^{\prime})\rVert=\lVert W_{l}x-W_{l}x^{\prime}\rVert=\lVert W_{l}(x-x^{\prime})\rVert\leq\lambda_{l}\lVert x-x^{\prime}\rVert.

(71)

Based on the above result, we expand the following in a lay-wise fashion:

\displaystyle\lVert f_{cls}(W;x+\Delta x)-f_{cls}(W;x)\rVert=\;

\displaystyle\lVert\circ_{l=1}^{L}(\sigma_{l}\circ W_{l}(x+\Delta x))-\circ_{l=1}^{L}(\sigma_{l}\circ W_{l}(x))\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle L_{\sigma_{L}}\lambda_{L}\lVert\circ_{l=1}^{L-1}(\sigma_{l}\circ W_{l}(x+\Delta x))-\circ_{l=1}^{L-1}(\sigma_{l}\circ W_{l}(x))\rVert\leq\cdots

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\left(\prod_{l=1}^{L}L_{\sigma_{l}}\lambda_{l}\right)\lVert x+\Delta x-x\rVert=\left(\prod_{l=1}^{L}L_{\sigma_{l}}\lambda_{l}\right)\lVert\Delta x\rVert.

(72)

Substituting
x+\Delta x=x^{\prime}
, Eq. (
69
) is resulted.
Under the assumption
\lVert f_{cls}(W;x)\rVert\leq 1
, Eq. (
70
) is a direct result of applying Theorem 1 of
14
, which is re-written as Theorem
J.7
in our appendix.
This completes the proof.
∎

Proposition E.4

(Truncated-spectrum vRKHS norm upper bound)
.

Under Assumption
D.1
, let
f\in\mathcal{H}_{K_{\ell}}
satisfy
f=\Pi_{r}f
.
Then the vRKHS norm of
f
admits the following bound:

\|f\|_{\mathcal{H}_{K_{\ell}}}^{2}\;\leq\;\frac{1}{\lambda_{r}}\,\|f\|_{L_{2}(\mu;\mathbb{R}^{C})}^{2}\;\leq\;\frac{C}{\lambda_{r}}\,\|f\|_{\infty}^{2},

(73)

where
\lambda_{r}
is the
r
-th eigenvalue of
T_{K_{\ell}}
, and
\|f\|_{\infty}:=\sup_{Z\in\mathcal{Z}}\|f(Z)\|_{\infty}
.

In particular, applying (
73
) to
f=\ell_{h,g_{D}}
yields

\|\ell_{h,g_{D}}\|_{\mathcal{H}_{K_{\ell}}}^{2}\;\leq\;\frac{C}{\lambda_{r}}\,\|\ell_{h,g_{D}}\|_{\infty}^{2},

(74)

which isolates a
spectral-geometry factor

\lambda_{r}^{-1}
and an
amplitude factor

\|\ell_{h,g_{D}}\|_{\infty}
.

Proof.

Since
f=\Pi_{r}f
, we can expand it in the first
r
eigenfunctions:

f(Z)=\sum_{i=1}^{r}a_{i}\psi_{i}(Z)
with coefficients
a_{i}\in\mathbb{R}
.
On the truncated space
\mathcal{H}_{K_{\ell}}^{(r)}=\mathrm{span}\{\psi_{1},\dots,\psi_{r}\}
, for any
f,g\in\mathcal{H}_{K_{\ell}}^{(r)}
we have
f=\sum_{i=1}^{r}a_{i}\psi_{i}
and
g=\sum_{i=1}^{r}a_{i}\psi_{i}
.
We endow the inner product
\langle f,g\rangle_{\mathcal{H}_{K_{\ell}}}=\langle\sum_{i}a_{i}\psi_{i},\sum_{i}b_{i}\psi_{i}\rangle_{\mathcal{H}_{K_{\ell}}}:=\sum_{i=1}^{r}a_{i}b_{i}/\lambda_{i}
.
Therefore
\|f\|_{\mathcal{H}_{K_{\ell}}}^{2}=\langle f,f\rangle_{\mathcal{H}_{K_{\ell}}}=\sum_{i=1}^{r}a_{i}^{2}/\lambda_{i}
.

By the standard characterization of the (vector-valued) RKHS norm via the eigensystem of
T_{K_{\ell}}
,

\|f\|_{\mathcal{H}_{K_{\ell}}}^{2}\;=\;\sum_{i=1}^{r}\frac{a_{i}^{2}}{\lambda_{i}}\;\leq\;\frac{1}{\lambda_{r}}\sum_{i=1}^{r}a_{i}^{2}.

(75)

On the other hand, orthonormality of
\{\psi_{i}\}
in
L_{2}(\mu;\mathbb{R}^{C})
implies

\|f\|_{L_{2}(\mu;\mathbb{R}^{C})}^{2}\;=\;\langle f,f\rangle\;=\;\langle\sum_{i=1}^{r}a_{i}\psi_{i},\sum_{i=1}^{r}a_{i}\psi_{i}\rangle\;=\;\sum_{i=1}^{r}\sum_{j=1}^{r}a_{i}a_{j}\langle\psi_{i},\psi_{j}\rangle\;=\;\sum_{i=1}^{r}a_{i}^{2}.

(76)

Combining (
75
) and (
76
) gives
\|f\|_{\mathcal{H}_{K_{\ell}}}^{2}\leq\lambda_{r}^{-1}\|f\|_{L_{2}(\mu;\mathbb{R}^{C})}^{2}
.

Finally, since
\mu
is a probability measure,

\|f\|_{L_{2}(\mu;\mathbb{R}^{C})}^{2}=\int_{\mathcal{Z}}\|f(Z)\|_{2}^{2}\,d\mu(Z)\leq\sup_{Z\in\mathcal{Z}}\|f(Z)\|_{2}^{2}\leq C\sup_{Z\in\mathcal{Z}}\|f(Z)\|_{\infty}^{2}=C\|f\|_{\infty}^{2},

where we used
\|v\|_{2}^{2}\leq C\|v\|_{\infty}^{2}
for
v\in\mathbb{R}^{C}
.
This proves (
73
), and (
74
) follows by taking
f=\ell_{h,g_{D}}
.
∎

Lemma E.5

(Hypothesis-Labeling Function Disagreement)
.

Follow the problem setting explained in the beginning of Section
3
and suppose Assumptions
C.8
,
C.10
,
C.11
, and
D.1
hold.
Then, the following holds:

\lVert\ell_{h,g_{D}}\rVert_{\mathcal{H}_{K_{\ell}}}^{2}\leq\frac{C}{\lambda_{r}}\left(L_{NN}\left\lVert\bar{h}_{G}(Z)-\bar{g}_{G_{D}}(Z)\right\rVert_{\infty}+G_{NN}\right)^{2},

(77)

where
\mathcal{H}_{K_{\ell}}
is the vRKHS space containing the hypothesis function
h
, the ground-truth labeling function
g_{D}
, and the loss mapping function
\ell_{h,g_{D}}
, also
L_{NN}=\prod_{l=1}^{L}L_{\sigma_{l}}\lambda_{l}
and
G_{NN}=\left(\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\kappa^{2}\right)^{L}\left(\prod_{l=1}^{L}\left(1+\frac{\lVert\Delta W_{l}\rVert_{F}}{\lVert W_{l}\rVert_{F}}\right)-1\right)
are MLP-specific constants.

Proof.

Given Proposition
E.4
, it suffices to analyze only
\lVert\ell_{h,g_{D}}(Z)\rVert_{\infty}
.
We now derive its upper bound by decomposing the hypothesis and labeling functions into feature extractors and classifiers.
Under the assumptions on the hypothesis family and labeling function, it has

\displaystyle\lVert\ell_{h,g_{D}}(Z)\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\left\lVert h_{cls}(\bar{h}_{G}(Z))-g_{cls_{D}}(\bar{g}_{G_{D}}(Z))\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\left\lVert h_{cls}(\bar{h}_{G}(Z))-h_{cls}(\bar{g}_{G_{D}}(Z))+h_{cls}(\bar{g}_{G_{D}}(Z))-g_{cls_{D}}(\bar{g}_{G_{D}}(Z))\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle L_{cls}\left\lVert\bar{h}_{G}(Z)-\bar{g}_{G_{D}}(Z)\right\rVert_{\infty}+\lVert h_{cls}(\bar{g}_{G_{D}}(Z))-g_{cls_{D}}(\bar{g}_{G_{D}}(Z))\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle L_{cls}\left\lVert\bar{h}_{G}(Z)-\bar{g}_{G_{D}}(Z)\right\rVert_{\infty}+\lVert g_{cls_{D}}(W+\Delta W;\bar{g}_{G_{D}}(Z))-g_{cls_{D}}(W;\bar{g}_{G_{D}}(Z))\rVert_{\infty},

(78)

where
L_{cls}
is the Lipschitz constant of the MLP classifier.
Applying result from Lemma
E.3
, i.e., Eqs. (
69
) and (
70
), it has

\lVert\ell_{h,g_{D}}(Z)\rVert_{\infty}\leq\left(\prod_{l=1}^{L}L_{\sigma_{l}}\lambda_{l}\right)\left\lVert\bar{h}_{G}(Z)-\bar{g}_{G_{D}}(Z)\right\rVert_{\infty}+\left(\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\kappa^{2}\right)^{L}\left(\prod_{l=1}^{L}\left(1+\frac{\lVert\Delta W_{l}\rVert_{F}}{\lVert W_{l}\rVert_{F}}\right)-1\right).

(79)

Finally, incorporating Eq. (
79
) into Eq. (
74
), Eq. (
77
) is resulted. This completes the proof.

∎

Appendix F
Bounding Convergence Error

In this section, we prove major lemmas that enable the proof of convergence error in Section
3.3
, which uses a basic result on recurrence inequality presented in Lem.
F.1
. Our primal idea is to use existing concentration inequalities to prove that the discrete samples converge to the continuous function from which they are drawn. In Lem.
F.2
, we prove that under the defined mean aggregation scheme, the difference between outputs of the discrete and continuous MPNNs is bounded. Then, we prove a layer-wise upper bound between the outputs of MPNN and cMPNN. The further two lemmas show bounds for outputs of MPNN and cMPNN with
l
-layer, respectively. Altogether, the above lemmas ultimately leads to Thm.
F.5
, providing the convergence error bound
\Delta_{N}
as in Eq. (
12
).

Lemma F.1

(Recurrence Inequality)
.

Given a sequence of real numbers
\{\Delta_{t}\in\mathbb{R}\ |\ t=0,1,\cdots,T\}
, when
\Delta_{t+1}\leq A^{(t+1)}\Delta_{t}+B^{(t+1)}
, the following holds

\Delta_{T}\leq\sum_{t=1}^{T}B^{(t)}\prod_{t^{\prime}=t+1}^{T}A^{(t^{\prime})}+\Delta_{0}\cdot\prod_{t=1}^{T}A^{(t)},

(80)

where we define
\prod_{t=T+1}^{T}A^{(t^{\prime})}=1
.

Proof.

We prove by induction. Denote the following statement by
P(n)
:

\Delta_{n}\leq\sum_{t=1}^{n}B^{(t)}\prod_{t^{\prime}=t+1}^{n}A^{(t^{\prime})}+\Delta_{0}\cdot\prod_{t=1}^{n}A^{(t)}.

(81)

1) Base Case.
When
n=1
,
P(1)
holds, i.e.,

\Delta_{1}\leq A^{(1)}\Delta_{0}+B^{(1)}=\sum_{t=1}^{n=1}B^{(t)}\prod_{t^{\prime}=1+1}^{1}A^{(t^{\prime})}+\Delta_{0}\cdot\prod_{t=1}^{n=1}A^{(t)},

(82)

by recurrence relation
\Delta_{1}\leq A^{(1)}\Delta_{0}+B^{(1)}
and by definition
\prod_{t^{\prime}=1+1}^{1}A^{(t^{\prime})}=1
.

2) Induction Hypothesis.
Suppose
P(k)
is correct, namely,

\Delta_{k}\leq\sum_{t=1}^{k}B^{(t)}\prod_{t^{\prime}=t+1}^{k}A^{(t^{\prime})}+\Delta_{0}\cdot\prod_{t=1}^{k}A^{(t)}.

(83)

3) Induction Step.
We aim at showing
P(k+1)
is correct, for which it has

\displaystyle\Delta_{k+1}

\displaystyle\leq A^{(t+1)}\Delta_{k}+B^{(k+1)}

(84)

\displaystyle\leq A^{(t+1)}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{k}B^{(t)}\prod_{t^{\prime}=t+1}^{k}A^{(t^{\prime})}+\Delta_{0}\cdot\prod_{t=1}^{k}A^{(t)}\right)+B^{(k+1)}

(85)

\displaystyle=\left(\sum_{t=1}^{k}B^{(t)}\prod_{t^{\prime}=t+1}^{k}A^{(t^{\prime})}+B^{(k+1)}\cdot 1\right)+\Delta_{0}\cdot\prod_{t=1}^{k+1}A^{(t)}

(86)

\displaystyle=\left(\sum_{t=1}^{k}B^{(t)}\prod_{t^{\prime}=t+1}^{k}A^{(t^{\prime})}+B^{(k+1)}\prod_{t^{\prime}=(k+1)+1}^{k+1}A^{(t^{\prime})}\right)+\Delta_{0}\cdot\prod_{t=1}^{k+1}A^{(t)}

(87)

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=1}^{k+1}B^{(t)}\prod_{t^{\prime}=t+1}^{k+1}A^{(t^{\prime})}+\Delta_{0}\cdot\prod_{t=1}^{k+1}A^{(t)},

(88)

where Eq. (
84
) holds by recurrence relation, Eq. (
85
) holds by induction hypothesis, Eq. (
86
) results from re-organizing terms, Eq. (
87
) is obtained from
\prod_{t^{\prime}=(k+1)+1}^{k+1}A^{(t^{\prime})}=1
, which altogether leads to Eq. (
88
) and shows that
P(k+1)
is correct.
So far we have proven by induction that if
P(1)
is correct, then
\forall n\in\mathbb{N}^{*}
,
P(n)
is correct.
This completes the proof.

∎

Lemma F.2
.

Given an RGM
\Gamma=(W,P,f)
and the discrete and continuous versions of an MPNN as in Def.
B.7
and Def.
B.8
, suppose Assumptions
C.1
,
C.2
,
C.4
, and
C.7
hold.
For
0<\rho<1
, define

\displaystyle C_{W}(\rho)=\;

\displaystyle L_{W}^{\infty}\left(\sqrt{\log(C_{\mathcal{X}})}+\sqrt{D_{\mathcal{X}}}\right)+\left(\sqrt{2}\left\lVert W\right\rVert_{\infty}+L_{W}^{\infty}\right)\sqrt{\log\left(\frac{2}{\rho}\right)},

(89)

\displaystyle C_{\Phi;f}=\;

\displaystyle L_{\Phi}\lVert f\rVert_{\infty}+\left\lVert\Phi(0,0)\right\rVert_{\infty}.

(90)

When a sufficiently large number of points is sampled following
P
in
\mathcal{X}
, i.e.,
\sqrt{N}>\frac{2C_{W}(\rho)}{d_{\min}}
, the following holds with a probability at least
1-2\rho
:

\displaystyle\left\lVert M_{X}(\cdot)-M_{W}(\cdot)\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{2C_{W}(\rho)C_{\Phi;f}\left\lVert W\right\rVert_{\infty}}{d_{\min}^{2}\sqrt{N}}+N^{-\frac{1}{2(D_{\mathcal{X}}+1)}}\left(\frac{2\left(\lVert W\rVert_{\infty}L_{\Phi}L_{f}^{\infty}+L_{W}^{\infty}C_{\Phi;f}\right)}{d_{\min}}+\right.

\displaystyle\left.\frac{\lVert W\rVert_{\infty}C_{\mathcal{X}}C_{\Phi;f}}{\sqrt{2}d_{\min}}\sqrt{\log(C_{\mathcal{X}})+\frac{D_{\mathcal{X}}\log(N)}{2(D_{\mathcal{X}+1})}+\log\left(\frac{2}{\rho}\right)}\right).

(91)

Proof.

Applying the definitions of discrete and continuous mean aggregation operators in
B.5
and
B.6
, we bound their difference by adding and removing the term
-\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\frac{W(\cdot,x_{i})}{d_{W}(\cdot)}\Phi(f(\cdot),f(x_{i}))
, and this gives

\displaystyle\left\lVert M_{X}(\cdot)-M_{W}(\cdot)\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle=\left\lVert\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\frac{W(\cdot,x_{i})}{d_{X}(\cdot)}\Phi(f(\cdot),f(x_{i}))-\int_{\mathcal{X}}\frac{W(\cdot,x)}{d_{W}(\cdot)}\Phi(f(\cdot),f(x))\,dP(x)\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\frac{W(\cdot,x_{i})}{d_{X}(\cdot)}\Phi(f(\cdot),f(x_{i}))-\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\frac{W(\cdot,x_{i})}{d_{W}(\cdot)}\Phi(f(\cdot),f(x_{i}))\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle+\left\lVert\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\frac{W(\cdot,x_{i})}{d_{W}(\cdot)}\Phi(f(\cdot),f(x_{i}))-\int_{\mathcal{X}}\frac{W(\cdot,x)}{d_{W}(\cdot)}\Phi(f(\cdot),f(x))\,dP(x)\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\underbrace{\left\lVert\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}W(\cdot,x_{i})\Phi(f(\cdot),f(x_{i}))\right\rVert_{\infty}}_{T_{1}}\underbrace{\left\lVert\frac{1}{d_{X}(\cdot)}-\frac{1}{d_{W}(\cdot)}\right\rVert_{\infty}}_{T_{2}}

(92)

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\left\lVert\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\tilde{W}(\cdot,x_{i})\Phi(f(\cdot),f(x_{i}))-\int_{\mathcal{X}}\tilde{W}(\cdot,x)\Phi(f(\cdot),f(x))\,dP(x)\right\rVert_{\infty}}_{T_{3}},

(93)

where
\tilde{W}(y,x)=\frac{W(y,x)}{d_{W}(y)}
.
We analyze below each term
T_{1}
,
T_{2}
and
T_{3}
,
separately.

We first bound
\left\lVert\Phi(f(x),f(y))\right\rVert_{\infty}
(with simplified notation as
\left\lVert\Phi(f,f)\right\rVert_{\infty}
) using its Lipschitz constant
L_{\Phi}
from Assumption
C.7
, resulting in

\displaystyle\lVert\Phi(f(x),f(y))\rVert_{\infty}=\;

\displaystyle\lVert\Phi(f(x),f(y))-\Phi(0,0)+\Phi(0,0)\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\left\lVert\Phi(f(x),f(y))-\Phi(0,0)\right\rVert_{\infty}+\left\lVert\Phi(0,0)\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle L_{\Phi}\left\lVert[f(x),f(y)]-[0,0]\right\rVert_{\infty}+\left\lVert\Phi(0,0)\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle L_{\Phi}\lVert f\rVert_{\infty}+\left\lVert\Phi(0,0)\right\rVert_{\infty}:=C_{\Phi;f}.

(94)

Applying the definition of infinity norm and Eq. (
F
), it then has

T_{1}\leq\left\lVert W\right\rVert_{\infty}\left\lVert\Phi(f,f)\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq\left\lVert W\right\rVert_{\infty}C_{\Phi;f}.

(95)

Now, we analyze
T_{2}
.
According to Assumption
C.2
, the RGM kernel degree is lower bounded by
d_{W}(\cdot)\geq d_{\min}
, which results in

T_{2}=\left\lVert\frac{d_{W}(\cdot)-d_{X}(\cdot)}{d_{X}(\cdot)d_{W}(\cdot)}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq\frac{d_{\min}^{-1}\left\lVert d_{X}(\cdot)-d_{W}(\cdot)\right\rVert_{\infty}}{\min_{x}|d_{X}(x)|},

(96)

and also

\displaystyle|d_{X}(\cdot)|

\displaystyle=|d_{X}(\cdot)-d_{W}(\cdot)+d_{W}(\cdot)|\geq|d_{W}(\cdot)|-|d_{X}(\cdot)-d_{W}(\cdot)|

\displaystyle\geq d_{\min}-\left\lVert d_{X}(\cdot)-d_{W}(\cdot)\right\rVert_{\infty}.

(97)

Applying Lemma
J.1
under Assumptions
C.1
and
C.2
with
f=1
, the following holds with a probability at least
1-\rho
:

\displaystyle\lVert d_{X}(\cdot)-d_{W}(\cdot)\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{L_{W}^{\infty}\left(\sqrt{\log(C_{\mathcal{X}})}+\sqrt{D_{\mathcal{X}}}\right)+\left(\sqrt{2}\left\lVert W\right\rVert_{\infty}+L_{W}^{\infty}\right)\sqrt{\log\left(\frac{2}{\rho}\right)}}{\sqrt{N}}

(98)

\displaystyle:=\frac{C_{W}(\rho)}{\sqrt{N}},

(99)

which is defined as event
\mathcal{E}_{1}
.
When the sample size is sufficiently large, i.e.,

N>4\left(\frac{C_{W}(\rho)}{d_{\min}}\right)^{2},

(100)

\lVert d_{X}(\cdot)-d_{W}(\cdot)\rVert_{\infty}\leq\frac{d_{\min}}{2}
holds, which, when being combined with Eq. (
F
), further results in

|d_{X}(\cdot)|\geq\frac{d_{\min}}{2}.

(101)

Combining the above equation with Eqs. (
96
) and (
98
), it has

T_{2}\leq 2d_{\min}^{-2}N^{-\frac{1}{2}}C_{W}(\rho),

(102)

Next, we proceed to analyzing
T_{3}
. By applying Lemma
J.3
under Assumptions
C.1
and
C.4
with
F_{y}(\cdot)=\tilde{W}(y,\cdot)\Phi(f(y),f(\cdot))
, the following holds with a probability at least
1-\rho
, as

T_{3}\leq N^{-\frac{1}{2(D_{\mathcal{X}}+1)}}\left(2L_{F_{y}}+\frac{C_{\mathcal{X}}\lVert F_{y}\rVert_{\infty}}{\sqrt{2}}\sqrt{\log(C_{\mathcal{X}})+\frac{D_{\mathcal{X}}\log(N)}{2(D_{\mathcal{X}+1})}+\log\left(\frac{2}{\rho}\right)}\right),

(103)

which is defined as the event
\mathcal{E}_{2}
.
It now boils down to the calculation of Lipschitz constant of
F_{y}(\cdot)
and its infinity norm
\lVert F_{y}(\cdot)\rVert_{\infty}
.
We analyze the Lipschitz constant
L_{\tilde{W}}^{\infty}
of
\tilde{W}(y,x)
with respect to
x
, as below

\displaystyle\left|\tilde{W}(y,x)-\tilde{W}(y,x^{\prime})\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\frac{W(y,x)}{d_{W}(x)}-\frac{W(y,x^{\prime})}{d_{W}(y)}\right|\leq d_{\min}^{-1}|W(y,x)-W(y,x^{\prime})|

\displaystyle\leq d_{\min}^{-1}L_{W}^{\infty}\|x-x^{\prime}\|_{\infty},

(104)

therefore we have
L_{\tilde{W}}^{\infty}\leq d_{\min}^{-1}L_{W}^{\infty}
, and
for
\forall x\in\mathcal{X},\ \left\lVert\tilde{W}(\cdot,x)\right\rVert_{\infty}=\left\lVert\frac{W(\cdot,x)}{d_{W}(\cdot)}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq d_{\min}^{-1}\left\lVert W\right\rVert_{\infty}

Then, with Eq. (
F
),
\forall x,x^{\prime}\in\mathcal{X}
, we have:

\displaystyle\lVert F_{y}(x)-F_{y}(x^{\prime})\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\left\lVert\tilde{W}(y,x)\Phi(f(y),f(x))-\tilde{W}(y,x^{\prime})\Phi(f(y),f(x^{\prime}))\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\left\lVert\tilde{W}(y,x)\Phi(f(y),f(x))-\tilde{W}(y,x)\Phi(f(y),f(x^{\prime}))\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle+\;

\displaystyle\left\lVert\tilde{W}(y,x)\Phi(f(y),f(x^{\prime}))-\tilde{W}(y,x^{\prime})\Phi\left(f(y),f(x^{\prime}))\right)\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\left\lVert\tilde{W}\right\rVert_{\infty}L_{\Phi}L_{f}^{\infty}\|x-x^{\prime}\|_{\infty}+L_{\tilde{W}}^{\infty}\|x-x^{\prime}\|_{\infty}\left\lVert\Phi(f,f)\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\left(d_{\min}^{-1}\lVert W\rVert_{\infty}L_{\Phi}L_{f}^{\infty}+d_{\min}^{-1}L_{W}^{\infty}C_{\Phi;f}\right)\|x-x^{\prime}\|_{\infty}.

(105)

Eq. (
105
) results from
\left\lVert\tilde{W}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq d_{\min}^{-1}\left\lVert W\right\rVert_{\infty}
,
L_{\tilde{W}}^{\infty}\leq d_{\min}^{-1}L_{W}^{\infty}
, and Eq. (
F
). It leads to

L_{F_{y}}\leq\frac{\lVert W\rVert_{\infty}L_{\Phi}L_{f}^{\infty}+L_{W}^{\infty}C_{\Phi;f}}{d_{\min}}.

(106)

Again, applying Eq. (
F
), for the infinity norm, we have

\lVert F_{y}(\cdot)\rVert_{\infty}=\left\lVert\tilde{W}(y,\cdot)\Phi(f(y),f(\cdot))\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq\left\lVert\tilde{W}\right\rVert_{\infty}\lVert\Phi(f,f)\rVert_{\infty}\leq\frac{\lVert W\rVert_{\infty}C_{\Phi;f}}{d_{\min}}.

(107)

Finally, substituting Eqs. (
106
) and (
107
) into Eq. (
103
), and combining the result with Eqs. (
95
), (
102
) to expand
\left\lVert M_{X}(\cdot)-M_{W}(\cdot)\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq T_{1}T_{2}+T_{3}
as in Eqs. (
92
) and (
93
), it results in Eq. (
F.2
).
Note that we use concentration inequalities twice for concluding the result. The event
\mathcal{E}_{1}
is regarding the number of nodes
N
such that the approximation error of non-normalized kernel is uniformly bounded. Likewise, the event
\mathcal{E}_{2}
is regarding the choice of
X_{N}
such that the approximation error of Monte-Carlo estimation towards the covering balls is uniformly bounded. Since each event holds independently with probability at least
1-\rho
, the final result holds with probability
(1-\rho)(1-\rho)=1-2\rho+\rho^{2}\geq 1-2\rho
.

∎

Lemma F.3
.

Given an RGM
\Gamma=(W,P,f)
and the discrete and continuous versions of an MPNN as in Def.
B.7
and Def.
B.8
, suppose Assumptions
C.2
and
C.7
hold.
Then, the output infinity norm and the Lipschitz constant of the cMPNN are bounded layer-wise by

\displaystyle\left\lVert f^{(l+1)}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq C_{1}^{(l+1)}+C_{2}^{(l+1)}\lVert f\rVert_{\infty},

(108)

\displaystyle L_{f^{(l)}}\leq D_{1}^{(l)}+D_{2}^{(l)}\lVert f\rVert_{\infty}+D_{3}^{(l)}L_{f}^{\infty},

(109)

where the constants are defined as follows:

\displaystyle C_{1}^{(l+1)}:=\;

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{l+1}\left(L_{\Psi^{(t)}}\lVert\Phi^{(t)}(0,0)\rVert_{\infty}+\lVert\Psi^{(t)}(0,0)\rVert_{\infty}\right)\prod_{l^{\prime}=t+1}^{l+1}L_{\Psi^{(l^{\prime})}}\left(1+L_{\Phi^{(l^{\prime})}}\right),

(110)

\displaystyle C_{2}^{(l+1)}:=\;

\displaystyle\prod_{t=1}^{l+1}L_{\Psi^{(t)}}\left(1+L_{\Phi^{(t)}}\right),

(111)

\displaystyle D_{1}^{(l)}:=\;

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{l}\Bigg[\left(L_{\Psi^{(t)}}L_{W}^{\infty}d_{\min}^{-1}\lVert\Phi^{(t)}(0,0)\rVert_{\infty}+L_{\Psi^{(t)}}\lVert\Phi^{(t)}(0,0)\rVert_{\infty}L_{W}^{\infty}d_{\min}^{-2}\right),

\displaystyle+C_{1}^{(t-1)}\left(L_{\Psi^{(t)}}L_{\Phi^{(t)}}L_{W}^{\infty}d_{\min}^{-1}+L_{\Psi^{(t)}}L_{\Phi^{(t)}}\lVert W\rVert_{\infty}L_{W}^{\infty}d_{\min}^{-2}\right)\Bigg]

\displaystyle\prod_{l^{\prime}=t+1}^{l}L_{\Psi^{(l^{\prime})}}\left(1+\lVert W\rVert_{\infty}d_{\min}^{-1}L_{\Phi^{(l^{\prime})}}\right),

(112)

\displaystyle D_{2}^{(l)}:=\;

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{l}C_{2}^{(t-1)}\left(L_{\Psi^{(t)}}L_{\Phi^{(t)}}L_{W}^{\infty}d_{\min}^{-1}+L_{\Psi^{(t)}}L_{\Phi^{(t)}}\lVert W\rVert_{\infty}L_{W}^{\infty}d_{\min}^{-2}\right)

\displaystyle\prod_{l^{\prime}=t+1}^{l}L_{\Psi^{(l^{\prime})}}\left(1+\lVert W\rVert_{\infty}d_{\min}^{-1}L_{\Phi^{(l^{\prime})}}\right),

(113)

\displaystyle D_{3}^{(l)}:=\;

\displaystyle 1+\lVert W\rVert_{\infty}d_{\min}^{-1}L_{\Phi^{(t)}}.

(114)

Proof.

We use result of Eq. (
F
), but layer-wise, i.e.,

\left\lVert\Phi^{(l)}\left(f^{(l-1)}(\cdot),f^{(l-1)}(\cdot)\right)\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq L_{\Phi^{(l)}}\left\lVert f^{(l-1)}\right\rVert_{\infty}+\left\lVert\Phi^{(l)}(0,0)\right\rVert_{\infty}=C_{\Phi^{(l)};f^{(l-1)}}.

(115)

By the definition of cMPNN and Eq. (
115
), we expand
\left\lVert f^{(l+1)}\right\rVert_{\infty}
as follows:

\displaystyle\left\lVert f^{(l+1)}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle=\left\lVert\Psi^{(l+1)}\left(f^{(l)}(\cdot),M_{W}^{\Phi^{(l+1)},f^{(l)}}(\cdot)\right)\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert\Psi^{(l+1)}\left(f^{(l)}(\cdot),M_{W}^{\Phi^{(l+1)},f^{(l)}}(\cdot)\right)-\Psi^{(l+1)}(0,0)\right\lVert_{\infty}+\left\lVert\Psi^{(l+1)}(0,0)\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq L_{\Psi^{(l+1)}}\left(\left\|f^{(l)}\right\|_{\infty}+\left\lVert M_{W}^{\Phi^{(l+1)},f^{(l)}}(\cdot)\right\rVert_{\infty}\right)+\left\lVert\Psi^{(l+1)}(0,0)\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle=L_{\Psi^{(l+1)}}\left(\left\|f^{(l)}\right\|_{\infty}+\left\lVert\int_{\mathcal{X}}\frac{W(\cdot,y)}{d_{W}(\cdot)}\Phi^{(l+1)}\left(f^{(l)}(\cdot),f^{(l)}(y)\right)\,dP(y)\right\rVert_{\infty}\right)+\left\lVert\Psi^{(l+1)}(0,0)\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq L_{\Psi^{(l+1)}}\left(\left\lVert f^{(l)}\right\rVert_{\infty}+\left\lVert\int_{\mathcal{X}}\frac{W(\cdot,y)}{d_{W}(\cdot)}\,dP(y)\right\rVert_{\infty}\cdot\left\lVert\Phi^{(l+1)}\left(f^{(l)}(\cdot),f^{(l)}(\cdot)\right)\right\rVert_{\infty}\right)+\left\lVert\Psi^{(l+1)}(0,0)\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq L_{\Psi^{(l+1)}}\left(\left\lVert f^{(l)}\right\rVert_{\infty}+L_{\Phi^{(l+1)}}\left\lVert f^{(l)}\right\rVert_{\infty}+\left\lVert\Phi^{(l+1)}(0,0)\right\rVert_{\infty}\right)+\left\lVert\Psi^{(l+1)}(0,0)\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle=L_{\Psi^{(l+1)}}\left(1+L_{\Phi^{(l+1)}}\right)\left\lVert f^{(l)}\right\rVert_{\infty}+L_{\Psi^{(l+1)}}\left\lVert\Phi^{(l+1)}(0,0)\right\rVert_{\infty}+\left\lVert\Psi^{(l+1)}(0,0)\right\rVert_{\infty}.

(116)

Applying Lemma
F.1
with

\displaystyle\Delta_{l+1}=\;

\displaystyle\left\lVert f^{(l+1)}\right\rVert_{\infty},

(117)

\displaystyle A^{(l+1)}=\;

\displaystyle L_{\Psi^{(l+1)}}\left(1+L_{\Phi^{(l+1)}}\right),

(118)

\displaystyle B^{(t+1)}=\;

\displaystyle L_{\Psi^{(l+1)}}\left\lVert\Phi^{(l+1)}(0,0)\right\rVert_{\infty}+\left\lVert\Psi^{(l+1)}(0,0)\right\rVert_{\infty},

(119)

Eq. (
108
) is obtained.
We proceed to proving (
109
). For
k=0,1,\cdots,l-1
and
\forall x,x^{\prime}\in\mathcal{X}
, applying Lipschitz constant definition, we have:

\displaystyle\left\lVert f^{(k+1)}(x)-f^{(k+1)}\left(x^{\prime}\right)\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\left\lVert\Psi^{(k+1)}\left(f^{(k)}(x),M_{W}^{\Phi^{(k+1)},f^{(k)}}(x)\right)-\Psi^{(k+1)}\left(f^{(k)}\left(x^{\prime}\right),M_{W}^{\Phi^{(k+1)},f^{(k)}}(x^{\prime})\right)\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle L_{\Psi^{(k+1)}}\left(\left\lVert f^{(k)}(x)-f^{(k)}\left(x^{\prime}\right)\right\rVert_{\infty}+\left\lVert M_{W}^{\Phi^{(k+1)},f^{(k)}}(x)-M_{W}^{\Phi^{(k+1)},f^{(k)}}(x^{\prime})\right\rVert_{\infty}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle L_{\Psi^{(k+1)}}\left(L_{f^{(k)}}\left\|x-x^{\prime}\right\|_{\infty}+\left\lVert M_{W}^{\Phi^{(k+1)},f^{(k)}}(x)-M_{W}^{\Phi^{(k+1)},f^{(k)}}(x^{\prime})\right\rVert_{\infty}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle L_{\Psi^{(k+1)}}L_{f^{(k)}}\left\|x-x^{\prime}\right\|_{\infty}+L_{\Psi^{(k+1)}}\underbrace{\left\lVert M_{W}^{\Phi^{(k+1)},f^{(k)}}(x)-M_{W}^{\Phi^{(k+1)},f^{(k)}}(x^{\prime})\right\rVert_{\infty}}_{T}

(120)

We focus on the second term, and it has

\displaystyle T=\;

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{\mathcal{X}}\left[\frac{W(x,y)}{d_{W}(x)}\Phi^{(k+1)}\left(f^{(k)}(x),f^{(k)}(y)\right)-\frac{W\left(x^{\prime},y\right)}{d_{W}\left(x^{\prime}\right)}\Phi^{(k+1)}\left(f^{(k)}\left(x^{\prime}\right),f^{(k)}(y)\right)\right]\,dP(y)\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\underbrace{\int_{\mathcal{X}}\left\|\frac{W(x,y)}{d_{W}(x)}\Phi^{(k+1)}\left(f^{(k)}(x),f^{(k)}(y)\right)-\frac{W\left(x^{\prime},y\right)}{d_{W}(x)}\Phi^{(k+1)}\left(f^{(k)}(x),f^{(k)}(y)\right)\right\|_{\infty}\,dP(y)}_{(A)}

\displaystyle+\;

\displaystyle\underbrace{\int_{\mathcal{X}}\left\|\frac{W\left(x^{\prime},y\right)}{d_{W}(x)}\Phi^{(k+1)}\left(f^{(k)}(x),f^{(k)}(y)\right)-\frac{W\left(x^{\prime},y\right)}{d_{W}(x)}\Phi^{(k+1)}\left(f^{(k)}\left(x^{\prime}\right),f^{(k)}(y)\right)\right\|_{\infty}\,dP(y)}_{(B)}

\displaystyle+\;

\displaystyle\underbrace{\int_{\mathcal{X}}\left\|\frac{W\left(x^{\prime},y\right)}{d_{W}(x)}\Phi^{(k+1)}\left(f^{(k)}\left(x^{\prime}\right),f^{(k)}(y)\right)-\frac{W\left(x^{\prime},y\right)}{d_{W}\left(x^{\prime}\right)}\Phi^{(k+1)}\left(f^{(k)}\left(x^{\prime}\right),f^{(k)}(y)\right)\right\|_{\infty}\,dP(y)}_{(C)},

(121)

for which we bound
(A)
,
(B)
and
(C)
separately as below.
For
(A)
:

\displaystyle(A)

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathcal{X}}\frac{\left|W(x,y)-W\left(x^{\prime},y\right)\right|}{d_{W}(x)}\left\|\Phi^{(k+1)}\left(f^{(k)}(x),f^{(k)}(y)\right)\right\|_{\infty}\,dP(y)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{L_{W}^{\infty}\|x-x^{\prime}\|_{\infty}}{d_{\min}}\int_{\mathcal{X}}\left\|\Phi^{(k+1)}\left(f^{(k)}(x),f^{(k)}(y)\right)\right\|_{\infty}\,dP(y)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{L_{W}^{\infty}\|x-x^{\prime}\|_{\infty}}{d_{\min}}\left\|\Phi^{(k+1)}\left(f^{(k)}(\cdot),f^{(k)}(\cdot)\right)\right\|_{\infty}\int_{\mathcal{X}}dP(y)

\displaystyle\leq L_{W}^{\infty}d_{\min}^{-1}\left(\left\|\Phi^{(k+1)}(0,0)\right\|_{\infty}+L_{\Phi^{(k+1)}}\left\|f^{(k)}\right\|_{\infty}\right)\|x-x^{\prime}\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq L_{W}^{\infty}d_{\min}^{-1}C_{\Phi^{(k+1)};f^{(k)}}\|x-x^{\prime}\|_{\infty}.

(122)

For
(B)
:

\displaystyle(B)

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathcal{X}}\left|\frac{W\left(x^{\prime},y\right)}{d_{W}(x)}\right|\left\|\Phi^{(k+1)}\left(f^{(k)}(x),f^{(k)}(y)\right)-\Phi^{(k+1)}\left(f^{(k)}\left(x^{\prime}\right),f^{(k)}(y)\right)\right\|_{\infty}\,dP(y)

\displaystyle\leq\|W\|_{\infty}d_{\min}^{-1}L_{\Phi^{(k+1)}}\int_{\mathcal{X}}\left\|\left[f^{(k)}(x),f^{(k)}(y)\right]-\left[f^{(k)}\left(x^{\prime}\right),f^{(k)}(y)\right]\right\|_{\infty}\,dP(y)

\displaystyle\leq\|W\|_{\infty}d_{\min}^{-1}L_{\Phi^{(k+1)}}\left\|f^{(k)}(x)-f^{(k)}\left(x^{\prime}\right)\right\|_{\infty}\leq\|W\|_{\infty}d_{\min}^{-1}L_{\Phi^{(k+1)}}L_{f^{(k)}}\|x-x^{\prime}\|_{\infty}.

(123)

For
(C)
:

\displaystyle(C)

\displaystyle=\left\|\frac{1}{d_{W}(x)}-\frac{1}{d_{W}(x^{\prime})}\right\|_{\infty}\int_{\mathcal{X}}\left\lvert W(x^{\prime},y)\right\rvert\left\lVert\Phi^{(k+1)}\left(f^{(k)}(x^{\prime}),f^{(k)}(y)\right)\right\rVert_{\infty}\,dP(y)

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\frac{d_{W}(x^{\prime})-d_{W}(x)}{d_{W}(x)d_{W}(x^{\prime})}\right\|_{\infty}\|W\|_{\infty}C_{\Phi^{(k+1);f^{(k)}}}

\displaystyle\leq\|W\|_{\infty}C_{\Phi^{(k+1);f^{(k)}}}d_{\min}^{-2}\int_{\mathcal{X}}\left\lvert W(x^{\prime},y)-W(x,y)\right\rvert\,dP(y)

\displaystyle\leq\|W\|_{\infty}C_{\Phi^{(k+1);f^{(k)}}}d_{\min}^{-2}\int_{\mathcal{X}}L_{W}^{\infty}\|x-x^{\prime}\|_{\infty}\,dP(y)

\displaystyle\leq\|W\|_{\infty}C_{\Phi^{(k+1)};f^{(k)}}d_{\min}^{-2}L_{W}^{\infty}\|x-x^{\prime}\|_{\infty}.

(124)

We can then substitute
(A)
,
(B)
and
(C)
into Eq. (
F
), and analyze the Lipschitz constant of
f^{(k+1)}(x)
.

\displaystyle\left\lVert f^{(k+1)}(x)-f^{(k+1)}\left(x^{\prime}\right)\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq L_{\Psi^{(k+1)}}L_{f^{(k)}}\|x-x^{\prime}\|_{\infty}+L_{\Psi^{(k+1)}}((A)+(B)+(C))

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle L_{\Psi^{(k+1)}}\left(L_{f^{(k)}}+L_{W}^{\infty}d_{\min}^{-1}C_{\Phi^{(k+1)};f^{(k)}}+\|W\|_{\infty}d_{\min}^{-1}L_{\Phi^{(k+1)}}L_{f^{(k)}}+\right.

\displaystyle\left.\|W\|_{\infty}C_{\Phi^{(k+1)};f^{(k)}}L_{W}^{\infty}d_{\min}^{-2}\right)\|x-x^{\prime}\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\left[L_{\Psi^{(k+1)}}\left(1+\|W\|_{\infty}d_{\min}^{-1}L_{\Phi^{(k+1)}}\right)L_{f^{(k)}}+\right.

\displaystyle\left.L_{\Psi^{(k+1)}}C_{\Phi^{(k+1)};f^{(k)}}L_{W}^{\infty}d_{\min}^{-1}(1+d_{\min}^{-1}\|W\|_{\infty})\right]\|x-x^{\prime}\|_{\infty},

(125)

which results in

L_{f^{(k+1)}}\leq L_{\Psi^{(k+1)}}\left(1+\|W\|_{\infty}d_{\min}^{-1}L_{\Phi^{(k+1)}}\right)L_{f^{(k)}}+L_{\Psi^{(k+1)}}C_{\Phi^{(k+1)};f^{(k)}}L_{W}^{\infty}d_{\min}^{-1}(1+d_{\min}^{-1}\|W\|_{\infty}).

(126)

Applying Lemma
F.1
with

\displaystyle\Delta_{l+1}=\;

\displaystyle L_{f^{(l+1)}},

(127)

\displaystyle A^{(l+1)}=\;

\displaystyle L_{\Psi^{(l+1)}}\left(1+\|W\|_{\infty}d_{\min}^{-1}L_{\Phi^{(l+1)}}\right),

(128)

\displaystyle B^{(t+1)}=\;

\displaystyle L_{\Psi^{(l+1)}}C_{\Phi^{(l+1)};f^{(l)}}L_{W}^{\infty}d_{\min}^{-1}(1+d_{\min}^{-1}\|W\|_{\infty}),

(129)

and re-organizing the formulation, Eq. (
109
) is resulted.
This completes the proof.

∎

Lemma F.4
.

Given an RGM
\Gamma=(W,P,f)
and the discrete and continuous versions of an MPNN as in Def.
B.7
and Def.
B.8
, suppose Assumptions
C.1
,
C.2
,
C.4
, and
C.7
hold.
Assess the layer-wise difference between the discrete and continuous versions of the MPNN through mean squared error (MSE), computed as

MSE_{X}\left(Z^{(l+1)},f^{(l+1)}\right)=\left(\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\lVert Z_{i}^{(l+1)}-\left(S_{X}f^{(l+1)}\right)_{i}\right\rVert_{\infty}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

(130)

Considering a graph signal sampled from the RGM, i.e.,
G\sim\Gamma
, to be used as the input of MPNN, the above difference is bounded by the following quantity with a probability at least
1-2\rho
:

\displaystyle MSE_{X}\left(Z^{(l+1)},f^{(l+1)}\right)

\displaystyle\leq N^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left(K_{1}+K_{2}\sqrt{\log\left(\frac{2}{\rho}\right)}+K_{3}\lVert f\rVert_{\infty}+K_{4}\lVert f\rVert_{\infty}\sqrt{\log{\left(\frac{2}{\rho}\right)}}\right)

\displaystyle+N^{-\frac{1}{2(D_{\mathcal{X}+1})}}(K_{5}+K_{6}\lVert f\rVert_{\infty}+K_{7}L_{f})

\displaystyle+N^{-\frac{1}{2(D_{\mathcal{X}+1})}}(K_{8}+K_{9}\lVert f\rVert_{\infty})\sqrt{\log(C_{\mathcal{X}})+\frac{D_{\mathcal{X}}\log(N)}{2(D_{\mathcal{X}+1})}+\log\left(\frac{2}{\rho}\right)}.

(131)

where the used constants are defined by

\displaystyle K_{1}:=\frac{2L_{W}^{\infty}\lVert W\rVert_{\infty}\left(\sqrt{\log(C_{\mathcal{X}})}+\sqrt{D_{\mathcal{X}}}\right)}{d_{\min}^{2}}\sum_{l=1}^{T}L_{\Psi^{(l)}}\left(L_{\Phi^{(l)}}C_{1}^{(l-1)}+\lVert\Phi^{(l)}(0,0)\rVert_{\infty}\right)\prod_{l^{\prime}=l+1}^{T}A^{(l^{\prime})},

\displaystyle K_{2}:=\frac{2\left(\sqrt{2}\left\lVert W\right\rVert_{\infty}+L_{W}^{\infty}\right)\lVert W\rVert_{\infty}}{d_{\min}^{2}}\sum_{l=1}^{T}L_{\Psi^{(l)}}\left(L_{\Phi^{(l)}}C_{1}^{(l-1)}+\lVert\Phi^{(l)}(0,0)\rVert_{\infty}\right)\prod_{l^{\prime}=l+1}^{T}A^{(l^{\prime})},

\displaystyle K_{3}:=\frac{2L_{W}^{\infty}\left(\sqrt{\log(C_{\mathcal{X}})}+\sqrt{D_{\mathcal{X}}}\right)\lVert W\rVert_{\infty}}{d_{\min}^{2}}\sum_{l=1}^{T}L_{\Psi^{(l)}}L_{\Phi^{(l)}}C_{2}^{(l-1)}\prod_{l^{\prime}=l+1}^{T}A^{(l^{\prime})},

\displaystyle K_{4}:=\frac{2\left(\sqrt{2}\left\lVert W\right\rVert_{\infty}+L_{W}^{\infty}\right)\lVert W\rVert_{\infty}}{d_{\min}^{2}\sqrt{N}}\sum_{l=1}^{T}L_{\Psi^{(l)}}L_{\Phi^{(l)}}C_{2}^{(l-1)}\prod_{l^{\prime}=l+1}^{T}A^{(l^{\prime})},

\displaystyle K_{5}:=\frac{2}{d_{\min}}\sum_{l=1}^{T}L_{\Psi^{(l)}}\left(2\lVert W\rVert_{\infty}L_{\Phi^{(l)}}D_{1}^{(l-1)}+L_{W}^{\infty}L_{\Phi^{(l)}}C_{1}^{(l-1)}+L_{W}^{\infty}\lVert\Phi^{(l)}(0,0)\rVert_{\infty}\right)\prod_{l^{\prime}=l+1}^{T}A^{(l^{\prime})},

\displaystyle K_{6}:=\frac{2}{d_{\min}}\sum_{l=1}^{T}L_{\Psi^{(l)}}\left(\lVert W\rVert_{\infty}L_{\Phi^{(l)}}C_{1}^{(l-1)}+L_{W}^{\infty}L_{\Phi^{(l)}}C_{2}^{(l-1)}\right)\prod_{l^{\prime}=l+1}^{T}A^{(l^{\prime})},

\displaystyle K_{7}:=\frac{2\lVert W\rVert_{\infty}}{d_{\min}}\sum_{l=1}^{T}L_{\Psi^{(l)}}L_{\Phi^{(l)}}D_{3}^{(l-1)}\prod_{l^{\prime}=l+1}^{T}A^{(l^{\prime})},

\displaystyle K_{8}:=\frac{C_{\mathcal{X}}\lVert W\rVert_{\infty}}{\sqrt{2}d_{\min}}\sum_{l=1}^{T}L_{\Psi^{(l)}}\left(L_{\Phi^{(l)}}C_{1}^{(l-1)}+\lVert\Phi^{(l)}(0,0)\rVert_{\infty}\right)\prod_{l^{\prime}=l+1}^{T}A^{(l^{\prime})},

\displaystyle K_{9}:=\frac{C_{\mathcal{X}}\lVert W\rVert_{\infty}}{\sqrt{2}d_{\min}}\sum_{l=1}^{T}L_{\Psi^{(l)}}L_{\Phi^{(l)}}C_{2}^{(l-1)}\prod_{l^{\prime}=l+1}^{T}A^{(l^{\prime})},

(132)

where
C_{1}^{(l-1)}
,
C_{2}^{(l-1)}
are defined in Eqs. (
110
) and (
111
), respectively;
D_{1}^{(l-1)}
,
D_{2}^{(l-1)}
and
D_{3}^{(l-1)}
are defined in Eqs. (
F.3
), (
F.3
) and (
114
), respectively; and
A^{(l)}
is defined as

A^{(l)}=L_{\Psi^{(l)}}\sqrt{1+\frac{8\lVert W\rVert_{\infty}^{2}L_{\Phi^{(l)}}^{2}}{d_{\min}^{2}}}.

(133)

Proof.

Applying the layer-wise structure of MPNN, it has
Z^{(l+1)}=h_{\Theta_{G}}^{(l+1)}\left(Z^{(l)}\right)
and
f^{(l+1)}=h_{\Theta_{W}}^{(l+1)}\left(f^{(l)}\right)
, based on which we analyze MSE error as follows:

\displaystyle MSE_{X}\left(Z^{(l+1)},f^{(l+1)}\right)=\;

\displaystyle\left(\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\lVert\left(h_{\Theta_{G}}^{(l+1)}\left(Z^{(l)}\right)\right)_{i}-\left(S_{X}h_{\Theta_{W}}^{(l+1)}\left(f^{(l)}\right)\right)_{i}\right\rVert_{\infty}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\left(\underbrace{\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\lVert\left(h_{\Theta_{G}}^{(l+1)}\left(Z^{(l)}\right)\right)_{i}-\left(h_{\Theta_{G}}^{(l+1)}\left(S_{X}f^{(l)}\right)\right)_{i}\right\rVert_{\infty}^{2}}_{T_{1}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle+\;

\displaystyle\left(\underbrace{\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\lVert\left(h_{\Theta_{G}}^{(l+1)}\left(S_{X}f^{(l)}\right)\right)_{i}-\left(S_{X}h_{\Theta_{W}}^{(l+1)}\left(f^{(l)}\right)\right)_{i}\right\rVert_{\infty}^{2}}_{T_{2}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}},

(134)

where we use
(\cdot)_{i}
to denote the
i
-th row of the input matrix. We summarize the result above as

MSE_{X}\left(Z^{(l+1)},f^{(l+1)}\right)\leq\sqrt{T_{1}}+\sqrt{T_{2}}.

(135)

When using a graph signal sampled from the RGM, i.e.,
G\sim\Gamma
, as the MPNN input, the adjacency weight satisfies
a_{ij}=W(x_{i},x_{j})
and the message signal in Eq. (
23
) becomes

m_{i}^{(l+1)}=\frac{1}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\frac{W(x_{i},x_{j})}{d_{X}(x_{i})}\Phi^{(l+1)}\left(z_{i}^{(l)},z_{j}^{(l)}\right),

(136)

which is denoted by
M_{G}^{\Phi^{(l+1)},Z^{(l)}}(x_{i})
to be consistent with the notation
M_{X}^{\Phi,f}(\cdot)
in Eq. (
20
).
Using the above expression of the MPNN message signal, we further bound the terms
T_{1}
and
T_{2}
separately as below.

By applying the Lipschitz continuity of
\Psi^{(l+1)}
and a simple norm inequality for a concatenated vector
z=[x,y]\in\mathbb{R}^{p+q}
with
x\in\mathbb{R}^{p}
and
y\in\mathbb{R}^{q}
, i.e.,
\lVert z\rVert_{\infty}\leq\lVert x\rVert_{\infty}+\lVert y\rVert_{\infty}
, we have

\displaystyle T_{1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\lVert\Psi^{(l+1)}\left(Z_{i}^{(l)},M_{G}^{\Phi^{(l+1)},Z^{(l)}}(x_{i})\right)-\Psi^{(l+1)}\left(\left(S_{X}f^{(l)}\right)_{i},M_{G}^{\Phi^{(l+1)},S_{X}f^{(l)}}(x_{i})\right)\right\rVert_{\infty}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{N}L_{\Psi^{(l+1)}}^{2}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\lVert Z_{i}^{(l)}-\left(S_{X}f^{(l)}\right)_{i}\right\rVert_{\infty}^{2}+\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\lVert M_{G}^{\Phi^{(l+1)},Z^{(l)}}(x_{i})-M_{G}^{\Phi^{(l+1)},S_{X}f^{(l)}}(x_{i})\right\rVert_{\infty}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=L_{\Psi^{(l+1)}}^{2}\left(MSE_{X}\left(Z^{(l)},f^{(l)}\right)^{2}+\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\underbrace{\left\lVert M_{G}^{\Phi^{(l+1)},Z^{(l)}}(x_{i})-M_{G}^{\Phi^{(l+1)},S_{X}f^{(l)}}(x_{i})\right\rVert_{\infty}^{2}}_{T_{3}}\right).

(137)

Now we analyze
T_{3}
below by expanding it using Eq. (
136
), and applying Eq. (
101
) and Lipschitz continuity of
\Psi^{(l+1)}
:

\displaystyle T_{3}=\;

\displaystyle\left\lVert\frac{1}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\frac{W(x_{i},x_{j})}{d_{X}(x_{i})}\Phi^{(l+1)}\left(Z_{i}^{(l)},Z_{j}^{(l)}\right)-\frac{1}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\frac{W(x_{i},x_{j})}{d_{X}(x_{i})}\Phi^{(l+1)}\left(\left(S_{X}f^{(l)}\right)_{i},\left(S_{X}f^{(l)}\right)_{j}\right)\right\rVert_{\infty}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N^{2}}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\left(\frac{W(x_{i},x_{j})}{d_{X}(x_{i})}\right)^{2}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\left\lVert\Phi^{(l+1)}\left(Z_{i}^{(l)},Z_{j}^{(l)}\right)-\Phi^{(l+1)}\left(\left(S_{X}f^{(l)}\right)_{i},\left(S_{X}f^{(l)}\right)_{j}\right)\right\rVert_{\infty}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{4\lVert W\rVert_{\infty}^{2}}{Nd_{\min}^{2}}\sum_{j=1}^{N}L_{\Phi^{(l+1)}}^{2}\left(\left\lVert Z_{i}^{(l)}-\left(S_{X}f^{(l)}\right)_{i}\right\rVert_{\infty}^{2}+\left\lVert Z_{j}^{(l)}-\left(S_{X}f^{(l)}\right)_{j}\right\rVert_{\infty}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\frac{4\lVert W\rVert_{\infty}^{2}L_{\Phi^{(l+1)}}^{2}}{d_{\min}^{2}}\left(\left\lVert Z_{i}^{(l)}-\left(S_{X}f^{(l)}\right)_{i}\right\rVert_{\infty}^{2}+MSE_{X}\left(Z^{(l)},f^{(l)}\right)^{2}\right).

(138)

Substituting (
138
) back to (
137
) and defining the quantity
A^{(l)}
in Eq. (
133
), we upper bound
T_{1}
in terms of the convergence error
\left(Z^{(l)},f^{(l)}\right)
, as below:

\displaystyle T_{1}\leq\;

\displaystyle L_{\Psi^{(l+1)}}^{2}MSE_{X}\left(Z^{(l)},f^{(l)}\right)^{2}+

\displaystyle\frac{4\lVert W\rVert_{\infty}^{2}L_{\Psi^{(l+1)}}^{2}L_{\Phi^{(l+1)}}^{2}}{d_{\min}^{2}}\cdot\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left(\left\lVert Z_{i}^{(l)}-\left(S_{X}f^{(l)}\right)_{i}\right\rVert_{\infty}^{2}+MSE_{X}\left(Z^{(l)},f^{(l)}\right)^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle L_{\Psi^{(l+1)}}^{2}MSE_{X}\left(Z^{(l)},f^{(l)}\right)^{2}+

\displaystyle\frac{4\lVert W\rVert_{\infty}^{2}L_{\Psi^{(l+1)}}^{2}L_{\Phi^{(l+1)}}^{2}}{d_{\min}^{2}}\left(MSE_{X}(Z^{(l)},f^{(l)})^{2}+MSE_{X}(Z^{(l)},f^{(l)})^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle L_{\Psi^{(l+1)}}^{2}\left(1+\frac{8\lVert W\rVert_{\infty}^{2}L_{\Phi^{(l+1)}}^{2}}{d_{\min}^{2}}\right)MSE_{X}\left(Z^{(l)},f^{(l)}\right)^{2}=\left(A^{(l+1)}MSE_{X}\left(Z^{(l)},f^{(l)}\right)\right)^{2}.

(139)

Next, we upper bound
T_{2}
as follows:

\displaystyle T_{2}=\;

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\bigg\lVert\Psi^{(l+1)}\left(\left(S_{X}f^{(l)}\right)_{i},M_{G}^{\Phi^{(l+1)},S_{X}f^{(l)}}(x_{i})\right)-\left(S_{X}\Psi\left(f^{(l)}(x_{i}),M_{W}^{\Phi^{(l+1)},f^{(l)}}(x_{i})\right)\right)_{i}\bigg\rVert_{\infty}^{2}

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\bigg\lVert\Psi^{(l+1)}\left(f^{(l)}(x_{i}),M_{X}^{\Phi^{(l+1)},f^{(l)}}(x_{i})\right)-\Psi\left(f^{(l)}(x_{i}),M_{W}^{\Phi^{(l+1)},f^{(l)}}(x_{i})\right)\bigg\rVert_{\infty}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{L_{\Psi^{(l+1)}}^{2}}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\lVert M_{X}^{\Phi^{(l+1)},f^{(l)}}(x_{i})-M_{W}^{\Phi^{(l+1)},f^{(l)}}(x_{i})\right\rVert_{\infty}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle L_{\Psi^{(l+1)}}^{2}\left\lVert M_{X}^{\Phi^{(l+1)},f^{(l)}}(\cdot)-M_{W}^{\Phi^{(l+1)},f^{(l)}}(\cdot)\right\rVert_{\infty}^{2}.

(140)

Applying the result in Lemma
F.2
with their mean aggregation operators
M_{X}(\cdot)
and
M_{W}(\cdot)
implemented for the specific functions
\Phi=\Phi^{(l+1)}
and
f=f^{(l)}
, the following holds with a probability at least
1-2\rho
:

\displaystyle\sqrt{T_{2}}\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{2L_{\Psi^{(l+1)}}C_{W}(\rho)C_{\Phi^{(l+1)};f^{(l)}}\left\lVert W\right\rVert_{\infty}}{d_{\min}^{2}\sqrt{N}}+

\displaystyle L_{\Psi^{(l+1)}}N^{-\frac{1}{2(D_{\mathcal{X}}+1)}}\left(\frac{2\lVert W\rVert_{\infty}L_{\Phi^{(l+1)}}L_{f^{(l+1)}}^{\infty}+2L_{W}^{\infty}C_{\Phi^{(l+1)};f^{(l+1)}}}{d_{\min}}+\right.

\displaystyle\left.\frac{\lVert W\rVert_{\infty}C_{\mathcal{X}}C_{\Phi^{(l+1)};f^{(l)}}}{\sqrt{2}d_{\min}}\sqrt{\log(C_{\mathcal{X}})+\frac{D_{\mathcal{X}}\log(N)}{2(D_{\mathcal{X}+1})}+\log\left(\frac{2}{\rho}\right)}\right)=B^{(l+1)},

(141)

where the derived upper bound of
\sqrt{T_{2}}
is denoted as
B^{(l+1)}
.

Finally, substituting Eqs. (
139
) and (
F
) into Eq. (
135
), we have:

MSE_{X}(Z^{(l+1)},f^{(l+1)})\leq A^{(l+1)}MSE_{X}\left(Z^{(l)},f^{(l)}\right)+B^{(l+1)}.

(142)

Applying Lemma
F.1
with
\Delta_{l+1}=MSE_{X}(Z^{(l+1)},f^{(l+1)})
, it has

MSE_{X}(Z^{(T)},f^{(T})\leq\sum_{l=1}^{T}B^{(l)}\prod_{l^{\prime}=l+1}^{T}A^{(l^{\prime})},

(143)

where
MSE_{X}(Z^{(0)},f^{(0)})=0
since
Z^{(0)}
is directly sampled from
f^{(0)}
using operator
S_{X}
.
As a result the following holds with
a probability at least
1-2\rho
:

MSE_{X}\left(Z^{(T)},f^{(T)}\right)\leq\sum_{l=1}^{T}L_{\Psi^{(l)}}C^{(l)}\prod_{l^{\prime}=l+1}^{T}A^{(l^{\prime})},

(144)

where

\displaystyle C^{(l)}=\;

\displaystyle\frac{2C_{W}(\rho)\left(L_{\Phi^{(l)}}\lVert f^{(l-1)}\rVert_{\infty}+\lVert\Phi^{(l)}(0,0)\rVert_{\infty}\right)\left\lVert W\right\rVert_{\infty}}{d_{\min}^{2}\sqrt{N}}

(145)

\displaystyle+\;

\displaystyle N^{-\frac{1}{2(D_{\mathcal{X}}+1)}}\left[\frac{2\lVert W\rVert_{\infty}L_{\Phi^{(l)}}L_{f^{(l-1)}}}{d_{\min}}+\frac{2L_{W}^{\infty}}{d_{\min}}\left(L_{\Phi^{(l)}}\lVert f^{(l-1)}\rVert_{\infty}+\lVert\Phi^{(l)}(0,0)\rVert_{\infty}\right)\right.

\displaystyle+\;

\displaystyle\left.\frac{\lVert W\rVert_{\infty}C_{\mathcal{X}}}{\sqrt{2}d_{\min}}\left(L_{\Phi^{(l)}}\lVert f^{(l-1)}\rVert_{\infty}+\lVert\Phi^{(l)}(0,0)\rVert_{\infty}\right)\sqrt{\log(C_{\mathcal{X}})+\frac{D_{\mathcal{X}}\log(N)}{2(D_{\mathcal{X}+1})}+\log\left(\frac{2}{\rho}\right)}\right].

Applying the result in Lemma
F.3
for upper bounding
\lVert f^{(l)}\rVert_{\infty}
and
L_{f^{(l)}}
and expanding
C_{W}(\rho)
based on its definition in Lemma
F.2
, the term
C^{(l)}
is further bounded by

\displaystyle C^{(l)}\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{2C_{W}(\rho)\left(L_{\Phi^{(l)}}\left(C_{1}^{(l-1)}+C_{2}^{(l-1)}\lVert f\rVert_{\infty}\right)+\lVert\Phi^{(l)}(0,0)\rVert_{\infty}\right)\left\lVert W\right\rVert_{\infty}}{d_{\min}^{2}\sqrt{N}}+

\displaystyle N^{-\frac{1}{2(D_{\mathcal{X}}+1)}}\left(\frac{2\lVert W\rVert_{\infty}L_{\Phi^{(l)}}}{d_{\min}}\left(D_{1}^{(l-1)}+D_{2}^{(l-1)}\lVert f\rVert_{\infty}+D_{3}^{(l-1)}L_{f}\right)+\right.

\displaystyle\frac{2L_{W}^{\infty}}{d_{\min}}\left(L_{\Phi^{(l)}}\left(C_{1}^{(l-1)}+C_{2}^{(l-1)}\lVert f\rVert_{\infty}\right)+\lVert\Phi^{(l)}(0,0)\rVert_{\infty}\right)+

\displaystyle\left.\frac{\lVert W\rVert_{\infty}C_{\mathcal{X}}}{\sqrt{2}d_{\min}}\left(L_{\Phi^{(l)}}\left(C_{1}^{(l-1)}+C_{2}^{(l-1)}\lVert f\rVert_{\infty}\right)+\lVert\Phi^{(l)}(0,0)\rVert_{\infty}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.\cdot\sqrt{\log(C_{\mathcal{X}})+\frac{D_{\mathcal{X}}\log(N)}{2(D_{\mathcal{X}+1})}+\log\left(\frac{2}{\rho}\right)}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{\left(2L_{W}^{\infty}\left(\sqrt{\log(C_{\mathcal{X}})}+\sqrt{D_{\mathcal{X}}}\right)+\left(\sqrt{2}\left\lVert W\right\rVert_{\infty}+L_{W}^{\infty}\right)\sqrt{\log\left(\frac{2}{\rho}\right)}\right)}{d_{\min}^{2}\sqrt{N}}

\displaystyle\cdot\frac{\left(L_{\Phi^{(l)}}\left(C_{1}^{(l-1)}+C_{2}^{(l-1)}\lVert f\rVert_{\infty}\right)+\lVert\Phi^{(l)}(0,0)\rVert_{\infty}\right)\left\lVert W\right\rVert_{\infty}}{d_{\min}^{2}\sqrt{N}}+

\displaystyle N^{-\frac{1}{2(D_{\mathcal{X}}+1)}}\left[\frac{2\lVert W\rVert_{\infty}L_{\Phi^{(l)}}}{d_{\min}}\left(D_{1}^{(l-1)}+D_{2}^{(l-1)}\lVert f\rVert_{\infty}+D_{3}^{(l-1)}L_{f}\right)+\right.

\displaystyle\frac{2L_{W}^{\infty}}{d_{\min}}\left(L_{\Phi^{(l)}}\left(C_{1}^{(l-1)}+C_{2}^{(l-1)}\lVert f\rVert_{\infty}\right)+\lVert\Phi^{(l)}(0,0)\rVert_{\infty}\right)+

\displaystyle\frac{\lVert W\rVert_{\infty}C_{\mathcal{X}}}{\sqrt{2}d_{\min}}\left(L_{\Phi^{(l)}}\left(C_{1}^{(l-1)}+C_{2}^{(l-1)}\lVert f\rVert_{\infty}\right)+\lVert\Phi^{(l)}(0,0)\rVert_{\infty}\right)

\displaystyle\cdot\left.\sqrt{\log(C_{\mathcal{X}})+\frac{D_{\mathcal{X}}\log(N)}{2(D_{\mathcal{X}+1})}+\log\left(\frac{2}{\rho}\right)}\right].

(146)

Finally, we substitute the above bound of
C^{(l)}
into Eq. (
144
), re-organize terms, and obtain the following:

\displaystyle MSE_{X}\left(Z^{(T)},f^{(T)}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\left(K_{1}+K_{2}\sqrt{\log\left(\frac{2}{\rho}\right)}+K_{3}\lVert f\rVert_{\infty}+K_{4}\lVert f\rVert_{\infty}\sqrt{\log{\left(\frac{2}{\rho}\right)}}\right)

\displaystyle+N^{-\frac{1}{2(D_{\mathcal{X}+1})}}(K_{5}+K_{6}\lVert f\rVert_{\infty}+K_{7}L_{f})

\displaystyle+N^{-\frac{1}{2(D_{\mathcal{X}+1})}}(K_{8}+K_{9}\lVert f\rVert_{\infty})\sqrt{\log(C_{\mathcal{X}})+\frac{D_{\mathcal{X}}\log(N)}{2(D_{\mathcal{X}+1})}+\log\left(\frac{2}{\rho}\right)}.

(147)

Let
T=l+1
, Eq. (
F.4
) is resulted. This completes the proof.

∎

Theorem F.5

(Convergence Error)
.

Given an RGM
\Gamma=(W,P,f)
and the discrete and continuous versions of an MPNN as in Def.
B.7
and Def.
B.8
, suppose Assumptions
C.1
,
C.2
,
C.4
, and
C.7
hold.
Then the following holds with a probability at least
1-2\rho
:

\left\lVert\bar{h}_{G}(Z)-\bar{h}_{W,P}(f)\right\rVert_{\infty}^{2}\leq\Delta_{N},

(148)

where

\displaystyle\Delta_{N}=\;

\displaystyle\frac{R_{1}+R_{2}\lVert f\rVert_{\infty}^{2}}{N}+\frac{S_{1}+S_{2}\lVert f\rVert_{\infty}^{2}+S_{3}L_{f}^{2}+(T_{1}+T_{2}\lVert f\rVert_{\infty}^{2})\log(N)}{N^{\frac{1}{D_{\mathcal{X}}+1}}}

(149)

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{R_{3}+R_{4}\lVert f\rVert_{\infty}^{2}}{N}+\frac{S_{4}+S_{5}\lVert f\rVert_{\infty}^{2}}{N^{\frac{1}{D_{\mathcal{X}}+1}}}\right)\log\left(\frac{2}{\rho}\right),

along with the following constants computed from the constants used in Lem.
F.4
:

\displaystyle R_{i}:=14K_{i}^{2},\textmd{ for }i=1,2,3,4,

\displaystyle S_{1}:=14K_{5}^{2}+14K_{8}^{2}\log(C_{\mathcal{X}})+56\left(D_{1}^{(T)}\right)^{2}+7C_{\mathcal{X}}^{2}\left(C_{1}^{(T)}\right)^{2}\log(C_{\mathcal{X}}),

\displaystyle S_{2}:=14K_{6}^{2}+14K_{9}^{2}\log(C_{\mathcal{X}})+56\left(D_{3}^{(T)}\right)^{2}+7C_{{\mathcal{X}}}^{2}\left(C_{2}^{(T)}\right)^{2}\log(C_{\mathcal{X}}),

\displaystyle S_{3}:=14K_{7}^{2}+56\left(D_{3}^{(T)}\right)^{2}L_{f}^{2},

\displaystyle S_{4}:=14K_{8}^{2}+7C_{\mathcal{X}}^{2}\left(C_{1}^{(T)}\right)^{2},

\displaystyle S_{5}:=14K_{9}^{2}+7C_{\mathcal{X}}^{2}\left(C_{2}^{(T)}\right)^{2},

\displaystyle T_{1}:=\left(14K_{8}^{2}+7C_{\mathcal{X}}^{2}\left(C_{1}^{(T)}\right)^{2}\right)\frac{D_{\mathcal{X}}}{2(D_{\mathcal{X}}+1)},

\displaystyle T_{2}:=\left(14K_{9}^{2}+7C_{\mathcal{X}}^{2}\left(C_{2}^{(T)}\right)^{2}\right)\frac{D_{\mathcal{X}}}{2(D_{\mathcal{X}}+1)}.

(150)

Proof.

Applying the definition of discrete and continuous pooling operations, it has

\displaystyle\left\lVert\bar{h}_{G}(Z)-\bar{h}_{W,P}(f)\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\left\lVert\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left(h_{G}^{(T)}(Z)\right)_{i}-\int_{\mathcal{X}}h_{W}^{(T)}\circ f(x)\,dP(x)\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\left\lVert\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left(h_{G}^{(T)}(Z)\right)_{i}-\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left(S_{X}h_{W}^{(T)}(f)\right)_{i}\right\rVert_{\infty}+

\displaystyle\left\lVert\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left(S_{X}h_{W}^{(T)}(f)\right)_{i}-\int_{\mathcal{X}}h_{W}^{(T)}\circ f(x)\,dP(x)\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\lVert Z^{(T)}_{i}-\left(S_{X}f^{(T)}\right)_{i}\right\rVert_{\infty}+\left\lVert\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}h_{W}^{(T)}\circ f(x_{i})-\int_{\mathcal{X}}h_{W}^{(T)}\circ f(x)\,dP(x)\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle MSE_{X}\left(Z^{(T)},f^{(T)}\right)+\underbrace{\left\lVert\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}h_{W}^{(T)}\circ f(x_{i})-\int_{\mathcal{X}}h_{W}^{(T)}\circ f(x)\,dP(x)\right\rVert_{\infty}}_{T}.

(151)

We apply Lem.
J.3
to bound
T
by letting
F(\cdot)=h_{W}^{(T)}(f)=h_{W}^{(T)}\circ f(\cdot)
, and apply Lem.
F.3
to obtain
\lVert h_{W}^{(T)}(f)\rVert_{\infty}\leq C_{1}^{(T)}+C_{2}^{(T)}\lVert f\rVert_{\infty}
and
L_{h_{W}^{(T)}(f)}\leq D_{1}^{(T)}+D_{3}^{(T)}\lVert f\rVert_{\infty}+D_{3}^{(T)}L_{f}
.
As a result, this gives

\displaystyle T\leq\;

\displaystyle N^{-\frac{1}{2(D_{\mathcal{X}}+1)}}\bigg[2\left(D_{1}^{(T)}+D_{3}^{(T)}\lVert f\rVert_{\infty}+D_{3}^{(T)}L_{f}\right)

\displaystyle+\;

\displaystyle\frac{C_{\mathcal{X}}}{\sqrt{2}}\left(C_{1}^{(T)}+C_{2}^{(T)}\lVert f\rVert_{\infty}\right)\sqrt{\log(C_{\mathcal{X}})+\frac{D_{\mathcal{X}}\log(N)}{2(D_{\mathcal{X}+1})}+\log\left(\frac{2}{\rho}\right)}\bigg].

(152)

Combining Eq. (
152
) and Lem.
F.4
that bounds
MSE_{X}\left(Z^{(T)},f^{(T)}\right)
, we derive as below an upper bound of
\left\lVert\bar{h}_{G}(Z)-\bar{h}_{W,P}(f)\right\rVert_{\infty}
based on Eq. (
151
), by further re-organizing and merging constant terms and by highlighting the number of nodes
N
and probability terms such as
\log(\frac{2}{\rho})
.
This results in the following:

\displaystyle\left\lVert\bar{h}_{G}(Z)-\bar{h}_{W,P}(f)\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle N^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left(K_{1}+K_{2}\sqrt{\log\left(\frac{2}{\rho}\right)}+K_{3}\lVert f\rVert_{\infty}+K_{4}\lVert f\rVert_{\infty}\sqrt{\log\left(\frac{2}{\rho}\right)}\right)

\displaystyle+\;

\displaystyle N^{-\frac{1}{2(D_{\mathcal{X}+1})}}(K_{5}+K_{6}\lVert f\rVert_{\infty}+K_{7}L_{f})

\displaystyle+\;

\displaystyle N^{-\frac{1}{2(D_{\mathcal{X}+1})}}(K_{8}+K_{9}\lVert f\rVert_{\infty})\sqrt{\log(C_{\mathcal{X}})+\frac{D_{\mathcal{X}}\log(N)}{2(D_{\mathcal{X}+1})}+\log\left(\frac{2}{\rho}\right)}

\displaystyle+\;

\displaystyle N^{-\frac{1}{2(D_{\mathcal{X}}+1)}}\bigg[2\left(D_{1}^{(T)}+D_{3}^{(T)}\lVert f\rVert_{\infty}+D_{3}^{(T)}L_{f}\right)

\displaystyle+\;

\displaystyle\frac{C_{\mathcal{X}}}{\sqrt{2}}\left(C_{1}^{(T)}+C_{2}^{(T)}\lVert f\rVert_{\infty}\right)\sqrt{\log(C_{\mathcal{X}})+\frac{D_{\mathcal{X}}\log(N)}{2(D_{\mathcal{X}+1})}+\log\left(\frac{2}{\rho}\right)}\bigg].

(153)

Finally, we upper bound
\left\lVert\bar{h}_{G}(Z)-\bar{h}_{W,P}(f)\right\rVert_{\infty}^{2}
by applying one version of Cauchy-Schwartz inequality
\left(\sum_{i=1}^{N}a_{i}\right)^{2}\leq N\sum_{i=1}^{N}a_{i}^{2}
, i.e., to compute the sum of the squared additive quantities from the right side of Eq. (
F
). This results in

\displaystyle\left\lVert\bar{h}_{G}(Z)-\bar{h}_{W,P}(f)\right\rVert_{\infty}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{14\left(K_{1}^{2}+K_{3}^{2}\lVert f\rVert_{\infty}^{2}\right)}{N}+\frac{14\left(K_{2}^{2}+K_{4}^{2}\lVert f\rVert_{\infty}^{2}\right)\log\left(\frac{2}{\rho}\right)}{N}+\frac{14\left(K_{5}^{2}+K_{6}^{2}\lVert f\rVert_{\infty}^{2}+K_{7}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\right)}{N^{\frac{1}{D_{\mathcal{X}}+1}}}

\displaystyle+\;

\displaystyle\frac{14\left(K_{8}^{2}+K_{9}^{2}\lVert f\rVert_{\infty}^{2}\right)\left(\log(C_{\mathcal{X}})+\frac{D_{\mathcal{X}}\log(N)}{2(D_{\mathcal{X}}+1)}+\log\left(\frac{2}{\rho}\right)\right)}{N^{\frac{1}{D_{\mathcal{X}}+1}}}

\displaystyle+\;

\displaystyle\frac{56\left(\left(D_{1}^{(T)}\right)^{2}+\left(D_{3}^{(T)}\right)^{2}\lVert f\rVert_{\infty}^{2}+\left(D_{3}^{(T)}\right)^{2}L_{f}^{2}\right)}{N^{\frac{1}{D_{\mathcal{X}}+1}}}

\displaystyle+\;

\displaystyle\frac{7C_{\mathcal{X}}^{2}\left(\left(C_{1}^{(T)}\right)^{2}+\left(C_{2}^{(T)}\right)^{2}\lVert f\rVert_{\infty}^{2}\right)\left(\log(C_{\mathcal{X}})+\frac{D_{\mathcal{X}}\log(N)}{2(D_{\mathcal{X}}+1)}+\log\left(\frac{2}{\rho}\right)\right)}{N^{\frac{1}{D_{\mathcal{X}}+1}}}:=\Delta_{N}.

(154)

The above gives rise to the definition of
\Delta_{N}
as in Eq. (
149
), after re-organizing terms.
This completes the proof.
∎

Appendix G
Bounding Optimization Error

This section presents proofs and supporting results for bounding the optimization error. Lem.
G.1
bounds the output change of cMPNN by perturbing the RGM kernel
W
, while Lem.
G.2
perturbs the RGM distribution
P
. Together, Lem.
G.1
and Lem.
G.2
form the RGM perturbation result in Thm.
G.3
. Then, Thm.
G.4
presents the weight perturbation results.
Finally, the optimization error results is the combination of the RGM and weight perturbation results, presented in Thm.
G.5
.

Lemma G.1

(RGM Kernel Perturbation)
.

Given an RGM
\Gamma=(W,P,f)
and its perturbation
\Gamma^{\prime}=(W_{\tau},P,f^{\prime})
by kernel deformation
\tau
, and the discrete and continuous versions of an MPNN as in Def.
B.7
and Def.
B.8
, suppose Assumptions
C.1
-
C.3
and
C.5
-
C.7
hold. Define the following constants:

\displaystyle C_{3}^{(T)}

\displaystyle:=\frac{d_{\min}+W_{\max}}{d_{\min}^{2}}\sum_{l=1}^{T}L_{\Psi^{(l)}}\left(L_{\Phi^{(l)}}\left(C_{1}^{(l-1)}+C_{2}^{(l-1)}\lVert f\rVert_{\infty}\right)+\left\lVert\Phi^{(l)}(0,0)\right\rVert_{\infty}\right)

\displaystyle\cdot\prod_{l^{\prime}=l+1}^{T}L_{\Psi^{(l^{\prime})}}\left(1+\frac{W_{\max}}{d_{\min}}\cdot L_{\Phi^{(l^{\prime})}}\right),

(155)

\displaystyle C_{4}^{(T)}

\displaystyle:=\prod_{l=1}^{T}L_{\Psi^{(l)}}\left(1+\frac{W_{\max}}{d_{\min}}\cdot L_{\Phi^{(l)}}\right).

(156)

Then, the hypothesis change induced by kernel deformation is bounded by the following:

\left\lVert\bar{h}_{W,P}(f)-\bar{h}_{W_{\tau},P}(f^{\prime})\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq C_{3}^{(T)}C_{\nabla w}\lVert\nabla\tau\rVert_{\infty}+C_{4}^{(T)}\lVert f-f^{\prime}\rVert_{\infty},

(157)

where
C_{\nabla w}
is defined in Assumption
C.3
.

Proof.

Applying the continuous pooling and cMPNN structure in Def.
B.8
, we expand the following:

\displaystyle\left\lVert\bar{h}_{W,P}(f)-\bar{h}_{W_{\tau},P}(f^{\prime})\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle=\left\lVert\int_{\mathcal{X}}h_{W}^{(T)}\circ f(x)\,dP(x)-\int_{\mathcal{X}}h_{W_{\tau}}^{(T)}\circ f^{\prime}(x)\,dP(x)\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{\mathcal{X}}\left\lVert h_{W}^{(T)}\circ f(x)-h_{W_{\tau}}^{(T)}\circ f^{\prime}(x)\right\rVert_{\infty}\,dP(x)\leq\left\lVert f^{(T)}-f^{(T)^{\prime}}\right\rVert_{\infty}

(158)

It now suffices to bound the quantity
\lVert f^{(l+1)}-f^{(l+1)^{\prime}}\rVert_{\infty}
as below:

\displaystyle\left\lVert f^{(l+1)}-f^{(l+1)^{\prime}}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle=\left\lVert h_{W,P}\left(f^{(l)}\right)-h_{W_{\tau},P}\left(f^{(l)^{\prime}}\right)\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\underbrace{\left\lVert h_{W,P}\left(f^{(l)}\right)-h_{W_{\tau},P}\left(f^{(l)}\right)\right\rVert_{\infty}}_{T_{a}}+\underbrace{\left\lVert h_{W_{\tau},P}\left(f^{(l)}\right)-h_{W_{\tau},P}\left(f^{(l)^{\prime}}\right)\right\rVert_{\infty}}_{T_{b}}.

(159)

for which we will bound below the two terms
T_{a}
and
T_{b}
separately.

Applying the definition of cMPNN in Def.
B.8
and Lipschitz continuity of
\Phi
and
\Psi
, it has

\displaystyle T_{a}=\;

\displaystyle\left\lVert\Psi^{(l+1)}\left(f^{(l)},M_{W}^{\Phi^{(l+1)},f^{(l)}}\right)-\Psi^{(l+1)}\left(f^{(l)},M_{W_{\tau}}^{\Phi^{(l+1)},f^{(l)}}\right)\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle L_{\Psi^{(l+1)}}\left\lVert M_{W}^{\Phi^{(l+1)},f^{(l)}}-M_{W_{\tau}}^{\Phi^{(l+1)},f^{(l)}}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle L_{\Psi^{(l+1)}}\bigg\lVert\int_{\mathcal{X}}\frac{W(x,y)}{d_{W}(x)}\Phi^{(l+1)}\left(f^{(l)}(x),f^{(l)}(y)\right)\,dP(y)-

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{X}}\frac{{W_{\tau}}(x,y)}{d_{W_{\tau}}(x)}\Phi^{(l+1)}\left(f^{(l)}(x),f^{(l)}(y)\right)\,dP(y)\bigg\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle L_{\Psi^{(l+1)}}\int_{\mathcal{X}}\left\lVert\frac{W(x,y)}{d_{W}(x)}-\frac{{W_{\tau}}(x,y)}{d_{W_{\tau}}(x)}\right\rVert_{\infty}\cdot\left\lVert\Phi^{(l+1)}\left(f^{(l)}(x),f^{(l)}(y)\right)\right\rVert_{\infty}\,dP(y)

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle L_{\Psi^{(l+1)}}\left(L_{\Phi^{(l+1)}}\left\lVert f^{(l)}\right\rVert_{\infty}+\left\lVert\Phi^{(l+1)}(0,0)\right\rVert_{\infty}\right)\underbrace{\int_{\mathcal{X}}\left\lVert\frac{W(x,y)}{d_{W}(x)}-\frac{{W_{\tau}}(x,y)}{d_{W_{\tau}}(x)}\right\rVert_{\infty}\,dP(y)}_{T_{c}},

(160)

where we recall that
\left\lVert\Phi^{(l+1)}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq L_{\Phi^{(l+1)}}\lVert f^{(l)}\rVert_{\infty}+\left\lVert\Phi^{(l+1)}(0,0)\right\rVert_{\infty}
as shown by Eq. (
F
) in the proof of Lem.
F.2
.
It now suffices to upper bound the last term
T_{c}
in (
160
), which we expand below:

\displaystyle T_{c}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{\mathcal{X}}\left\lVert\frac{W(x,y)}{d_{W}(x)}-\frac{W_{\tau}(x,y)}{d_{W}(x)}\right\rVert_{\infty}\,dP(y)+\int_{\mathcal{X}}\left\lVert\frac{W_{\tau}(x,y)}{d_{W}(x)}-\frac{{W_{\tau}}(x,y)}{d_{W_{\tau}}(x)}\right\rVert_{\infty}\,dP(y)

\displaystyle=\left\lVert\frac{1}{d_{W}(x)}\right\rVert_{\infty}\int_{\mathcal{X}}\left\lVert W(x,y)-W_{\tau}(x,y)\right\rVert_{\infty}\,dP(y)+\lVert W_{\tau}\rVert_{\infty}\left\lVert\frac{1}{d_{W}(x)}-\frac{1}{d_{W_{\tau}}(x)}\right\rVert_{\infty}.

(161)

Applying a direct result from
59
used for deriving their Eq. (31), it has

\int_{\mathcal{X}}\left\lVert W(x,y)-W_{\tau}(x,y)\right\rVert_{\infty}\,dP(y)\leq C_{\nabla w}\lVert\nabla\tau\rVert_{\infty}.

(162)

Regarding to the second term in Eq. (
161
), it has

\displaystyle\left\lVert\frac{1}{d_{W}(x)}-\frac{1}{d_{W_{\tau}}(x)}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle=\left\lVert\frac{d_{W_{\tau}}(x)-d_{W}(x)}{d_{W}(x)d_{W_{\tau}}(x)}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert\frac{1}{d_{W}(x)d_{W_{\tau}}(x)}\right\rVert_{\infty}\cdot\int\left\lVert W_{\tau}(x,y)-W(x,y)\right\rVert_{\infty}\,dP(y)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{\nabla w}\lVert\nabla\tau\rVert_{\infty}}{d_{\min}^{2}}.

(163)

Substituting these back to Eq. (
161
), it has

T_{c}\leq\left\lVert\frac{1}{d_{W}(x)}\right\rVert_{\infty}C_{\nabla w}\lVert\nabla\tau\rVert_{\infty}+\frac{C_{\nabla w}\lVert\nabla\tau\rVert_{\infty}\lVert W_{\tau}\rVert_{\infty}}{d_{\min}^{2}}\leq\frac{C_{\nabla w}\lVert\nabla\tau\rVert_{\infty}}{d_{\min}}+\frac{C_{\nabla w}\lVert\nabla\tau\rVert_{\infty}W_{\max}}{d_{\min}^{2}}.

(164)

Substitute further Eq. (
164
) back to (
160
) and applying the result of Lem.
F.3
for bounding
\left\lVert f^{(l)}\right\rVert_{\infty}
, we conclude the bound of
T_{a}
in Eq. (
159
):

\displaystyle T_{a}\leq\;

\displaystyle L_{\Psi^{(l+1)}}\left(L_{\Phi^{(l+1)}}\left\lVert f^{(l)}\right\rVert_{\infty}+\left\lVert\Phi^{(l+1)}(0,0)\right\rVert_{\infty}\right)\frac{C_{\nabla w}\lVert\nabla\tau\rVert_{\infty}}{d_{\min}}\cdot\bigg(1+\frac{W_{\max}}{d_{\min}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle L_{\Psi^{(l+1)}}\left(L_{\Phi^{(l+1)}}\left(C_{1}^{(l)}+C_{2}^{(l)}\lVert f\rVert_{\infty}\right)+\left\lVert\Phi^{(l+1)}(0,0)\right\rVert_{\infty}\right)C_{\nabla w}\lVert\nabla\tau\rVert_{\infty}\left(\frac{1}{d_{\min}}+\frac{W_{\max}}{d_{\min}^{2}}\right).

(165)

We now seek to bound the second term
T_{b}
in Eq. (
159
) by expanding based on definition of cMPNN in Def.
B.8
:

\displaystyle T_{b}=\;

\displaystyle\bigg\lVert\Psi^{(l+1)}\left(f^{(l)},M_{W_{\tau}}^{\Phi^{(l+1)},f^{(l)}}\right)-\Psi^{(l+1)}\left(f^{(l)^{\prime}},M_{W_{\tau}}^{\Phi^{(l+1)},f^{(l)^{\prime}}}\right)\bigg\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle L_{\Psi^{(l+1)}}\left(\left\lVert f^{(l)}-f^{(l)^{\prime}}\right\rVert_{\infty}+\underbrace{\left\lVert M_{W_{\tau}}^{\Phi^{(l+1)},f^{(l)}}-M_{W_{\tau}}^{\Phi^{(l+1)},f^{(l)^{\prime}}}\right\rVert_{\infty}}_{T_{d}}\right)

(166)

It now suffices to bound the second term
T_{d}
in (
166
) as below:

\displaystyle T_{d}\leq\;

\displaystyle\bigg\lVert\int_{\mathcal{X}}\frac{W_{\tau}(x,y)}{d_{W_{\tau}}(x)}\Phi^{(l+1)}\left(f^{(l)}(x),f^{(l)}(y)\right)\,dP(y)-

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{X}}\frac{{W_{\tau}}(x,y)}{d_{W_{\tau}}(x)}\Phi^{(l+1)}\left(f^{(l)^{\prime}}(x),f^{(l)^{\prime}}(y)\right)\,dP(y)\bigg\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{W_{\max}}{d_{\min}}\left\lVert\Phi^{(l+1)}\left(f^{(l)},f^{(l)}\right)-\Phi^{(l+1)}\left(f^{(l)^{\prime}},f^{(l)^{\prime}}\right)\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq\frac{W_{\max}L_{\Phi^{(l+1)}}}{d_{\min}}\left\lVert f^{(l)}-(f^{(l)})^{\prime}\right\rVert_{\infty}.

(167)

Substituting the above back to Eq. (
159
), it has

\displaystyle T_{b}\leq\;

\displaystyle L_{\Psi^{(l+1)}}\left(\left\lVert f^{(l)}-f^{(l)^{\prime}}\right\rVert_{\infty}+\frac{W_{\max}L_{\Phi^{(l+1)}}}{d_{\min}}\left\lVert f^{(l)}-f^{(l)^{\prime}}\right\rVert_{\infty}\right)

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle L_{\Psi^{(l+1)}}\left(1+\frac{W_{\max}L_{\Phi^{(l+1)}}}{d_{\min}}\right)\left\lVert f^{(l)}-f^{(l)^{\prime}}\right\rVert_{\infty}.

(168)

Combining (
165
) and (
G
) into Eq. (
159
), we get the following recursion form:

\displaystyle\left\lVert f^{(l+1)}-f^{(l+1)^{\prime}}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq L_{\Psi^{(l+1)}}\left(L_{\Phi^{(l+1)}}\left(C_{1}^{(l)}+C_{2}^{(l)}\lVert f\rVert_{\infty}\right)+\left\lVert\Phi^{(l+1)}(0,0)\right\rVert_{\infty}\right)

\displaystyle\cdot C_{\nabla w}\lVert\nabla\tau\rVert_{\infty}\bigg(\frac{1}{d_{\min}}+\frac{W_{\max}}{d_{\min}^{2}}\bigg)

\displaystyle+L_{\Psi^{(l+1)}}\left(1+\frac{W_{\max}L_{\Phi^{(l+1)}}}{d_{\min}}\right)\left\lVert f^{(l)}-f^{(l)}\right\rVert_{\infty}

(169)

Applying the recursion Lem.
F.1
with
\Delta_{t}=\left\lVert f^{(t)}-f^{(t)^{\prime}}\right\rVert_{\infty}
, an upper bound for
\left\lVert f^{(T)}-f^{(T)^{\prime}}\right\rVert_{\infty}
is obtained, which, together with Eq. (
158
), results in Eq. (
157
).
This completes the proof.

∎

Lemma G.2

(RGM Distribution Perturbation)
.

Given an RGM
\Gamma=(W,P,f)
and its perturbation
\Gamma^{\prime}=(W,P_{\tau},f^{\prime})
by distribution deformation
\tau
, and the discrete and continuous versions of an MPNN as in Def.
B.7
and Def.
B.8
, suppose Assumptions
C.1
-
C.3
and
C.5
-
C.7
hold.
Define the following constants:

\displaystyle C_{4}^{(T)}

\displaystyle:=\prod_{l=1}^{T}L_{\Psi^{(l)}}\left(1+\frac{W_{\max}L_{\Phi^{(l)}}}{d_{\min}}\right),

\displaystyle C_{5}^{(T)}

\displaystyle:=C_{1}^{(T)}+C_{2}^{(T)}\lVert f\rVert_{\infty}.

(170)

Then, the hypothesis change induced by distribution deformation is bounded by the following:

\left\lVert\bar{h}_{W,P}(f)-\bar{h}_{W,P_{\tau}}(f^{\prime})\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq C_{5}^{(T)}N_{P_{\tau}}+C_{4}^{(T)}C_{P_{\tau}}\lVert f-f^{\prime}\rVert_{\infty},

(171)

where
N_{P_{\tau}}
and
C_{P_{\tau}}
are defined in Assumption
C.5
.

Proof.

Applying the continuous pooling operation and cMPNN structure in Def.
B.8
, the fact that
N_{P_{\tau}}=\|q_{\tau}(x)-1\|_{\infty}
and

q_{\tau}=\frac{dP_{\tau}}{dP}(x)\leq C_{P_{\tau}}
as in Assumption
C.5
, and the result of Lem.
F.3
for bounding
\left\lVert f^{(l)}\right\rVert_{\infty}
, we expand the following:

\displaystyle\left\lVert\bar{h}_{W,P}(f)-\bar{h}_{W,P_{\tau}}(f^{\prime})\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\left\lVert\bar{h}_{W,P}(f)-\bar{h}_{W,P_{\tau}}(f)\right\rVert_{\infty}+\left\lVert\bar{h}_{W,P_{\tau}}(f)-\bar{h}_{W,P_{\tau}}(f^{\prime})\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\left\lVert\int_{\mathcal{X}}h^{(T)}\circ f(x)\,dP(x)-\int_{\mathcal{X}}h^{(T)}\circ f(x)\,dP_{\tau}(x)\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle+\;

\displaystyle\left\lVert\int_{\mathcal{X}}h^{(T)}\circ f(x)\,dP_{\tau}(x)-\int_{\mathcal{X}}h^{(T)}\circ f^{\prime}(x)\,dP_{\tau}(x)\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\left\lVert\int_{\mathcal{X}}f^{(T)}(x)\cdot\left(q_{\tau}(x)-1\right)\,dP(x)\right\rVert_{\infty}+\int_{\mathcal{X}}\left\lVert f^{(T)}(x)-f^{(T)^{\prime}}(x)\right\rVert_{\infty}\,dP_{\tau}(x)

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\left\lVert q_{\tau}(x)-1\right\rVert_{\infty}\cdot\left\lVert f^{(T)}\right\rVert_{\infty}+\int_{\mathcal{X}}\left\lVert f^{(T)}(x)-f^{(T)^{\prime}}(x)\right\rVert_{\infty}q_{\tau}\,dP(x)

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle N_{P_{\tau}}\left(C_{1}^{(T)}+C_{2}^{(T)}\lVert f\rVert_{\infty}\right)+C_{P_{\tau}}\left\lVert f^{(T)}-f^{(T)^{\prime}}\right\rVert_{\infty}.

(172)

We have shown in the proof of Lemma
G.1
on bounding
\lVert f^{(l+1)}-(f^{(l+1)})^{\prime}\rVert_{\infty}
, i.e., Eq. (
G
).
This then results in an upper bound for
\left\lVert f^{(T)}-f^{(T)^{\prime}}\right\rVert_{\infty}
by applying the recursion Lem.
F.1
with
\Delta_{t}=\left\lVert f^{(t)}-f^{(t)^{\prime}}\right\rVert_{\infty}
, i.e.,

\left\lVert f^{(T)}-f^{(T)^{\prime}}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq C_{4}\lVert f-f^{\prime}\rVert_{\infty}.

(173)

Substituting the above into Eq. (
172
), Eq. (
171
) is obtained. This completes the proof.

∎

Theorem G.3

(RGM Perturbation)
.

Given an RGM
\Gamma=(W,P,f)
and its perturbation
\Gamma^{\prime}=(W_{\tau},P_{\tau},f^{\prime})
by both kernel and distribution deformation, and an MPNN under mean aggregation, suppose Assumptions
C.1
-
C.3
and
C.5
-
C.7
hold.
Then, the hypothesis change induced by the deformation is bounded by the following:

\displaystyle\lVert\bar{h}_{W,P}(f)-\bar{h}_{W_{\tau},P_{\tau}}(f^{\prime})\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq C_{3}C_{\nabla w}\lVert\nabla\tau\rVert_{\infty}+C_{4}(1+C_{P_{\tau}})\lVert f-f^{\prime}\rVert_{\infty}+C_{5}N_{P_{\tau}}

(174)

Proof.

Expanding the l.h.s. of Eq. (
174
) and applying results from Lem.
G.1
and
G.2
, it has

\displaystyle\left\lVert\bar{h}_{W,P}(f)-\bar{h}_{W_{\tau},P_{\tau}}(f^{\prime})\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert\bar{h}_{W,P}(f)-\bar{h}_{W_{\tau},P}(f^{\prime})\right\rVert_{\infty}+\left\lVert\bar{h}_{W_{\tau},P}(f^{\prime})-\bar{h}_{W_{\tau},P_{\tau}}(f^{\prime})\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq C_{3}C_{\nabla w}\lVert\nabla\tau\rVert_{\infty}+C_{4}\lVert f-f^{\prime}\rVert_{\infty}+C_{5}N_{P_{\tau}}+C_{4}C_{P_{\tau}}\lVert f-f^{\prime}\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle=C_{3}C_{\nabla w}\lVert\nabla\tau\rVert_{\infty}+C_{4}(1+C_{P_{\tau}})\lVert f-f^{\prime}\rVert_{\infty}+C_{5}N_{P_{\tau}}.

(175)

This completes the proof.

∎

Theorem G.4

(Weight Perturbation)
.

Given an RGM
\Gamma=(W,P,f)
and the discrete and continuous versions of an MPNN as in Def.
B.7
and Def.
B.8
, where the message and update functions are instantiated as
L
-layer MLPs as in Def
B.9
. Perturb the MPNN weight matrices by Def.
B.10
. Suppose Assumption
C.9
holds.
Then, the hypothesis change induced by weight perturbation is bounded by

\left\lVert\bar{h}_{W,P}(f)-\widetilde{\bar{h}}_{W,P}(f)\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq\sum_{l=1}^{T}\left(C_{T_{2}}^{(l)}+C_{T_{4}}^{(l)}\right)\prod_{l^{\prime}=l+1}^{T}\left(L_{\Psi^{(l^{\prime})}}+C_{T_{3}}^{(l^{\prime})}\right).

(176)

where the layer-specific constants are computed by

\displaystyle C_{\Phi}^{(l+1)}:=L_{\Phi^{(l+1)}}\left(C_{1}^{(l)}+C_{2}^{(l)}\left\lVert f\right\rVert_{\infty}\right)+\left\lVert\Phi^{(l+1)}(0,0)\right\rVert_{\infty},

(177)

\displaystyle\tilde{C_{1}}^{(l)}:=\sum_{t=1}^{l}\left(L_{\tilde{\Psi}^{(t)}}D_{\tilde{\Phi}^{(t)}(0,0)}+D_{\tilde{\Psi}^{(t)}(0,0)}\right)\prod_{l^{\prime}=t+1}^{l}L_{\tilde{\Psi}^{(l^{\prime})}}\left(1+L_{\tilde{\Phi}^{(l^{\prime})}}\right),

(178)

\displaystyle\tilde{C_{2}}^{(l)}:=\prod_{t=1}^{l}L_{\tilde{\Psi}^{(t)}}\left(1+L_{\tilde{\Phi}^{(t)}}\right),

(179)

\displaystyle\tilde{C}_{\Phi}^{(l+1)}:=L_{\Phi^{(l+1)}}\left(\tilde{C_{1}}^{(l)}+\tilde{C_{2}}^{(l)}\lVert f\rVert_{\infty}\right)+\left\lVert\Phi^{(l+1)}(0,0)\right\rVert_{\infty},

(180)

\displaystyle C_{T_{2}}^{(l+1)}:=L_{\Psi^{(l+1)}}\Delta_{\Phi}^{(l+1)}C_{\Phi}^{(l+1)}\frac{\lVert W\rVert_{\infty}}{d_{\min}},

(181)

\displaystyle C_{T_{3}}^{(l+1)}:=L_{\Psi^{(l+1)}}L_{\tilde{\Phi}^{(l)}}\frac{\lVert W\rVert_{\infty}}{d_{\min}},

(182)

\displaystyle\tilde{C_{5}}^{(l+1)}:=\max\left(\tilde{C_{1}}^{(l+1)}+\tilde{C_{2}}^{(l+1)}\lVert f\rVert_{\infty},\frac{\left\lVert W\right\rVert_{\infty}}{d_{\min}}\left(\Delta_{\Phi}^{(l+1)}+1\right)\tilde{C}_{\Phi}^{(l+1)}\right),

(183)

\displaystyle C_{T_{4}}^{(l+1)}:=\Delta_{\Psi}^{(l+1)}L_{\Psi^{(l+1)}}\tilde{C_{5}}^{(l)}+\Delta_{\Psi}^{(l+1)}\left\lVert\Psi^{(l+1)}(0,0)\right\rVert_{\infty},

(184)

with

\displaystyle\Delta_{\Phi}^{(l+1)}=\sqrt{H_{l+1}}\left(\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\kappa^{2}\right)^{L}\left[\prod_{k=1}^{L}\left(1+\frac{\left\lVert\Delta\Theta_{\Phi_{k}^{(l+1)}}\right\rVert_{F}}{\left\lVert\Theta_{\Phi_{k}^{(l+1)}}\right\rVert_{F}}\right)-1\right],

(185)

\displaystyle\Delta_{\Psi}^{(l+1)}=\sqrt{F_{l+1}}\left(\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\kappa^{2}\right)^{L}\left[\prod_{k=1}^{L}\left(1+\frac{\lVert\Delta\Theta_{\Psi_{k}^{(l+1)}}\rVert_{F}}{\lVert\Theta_{\Psi_{k}^{(l+1)}}\rVert_{F}}\right)-1\right],

(186)

\displaystyle L_{\tilde{\Psi}^{(l)}}=L_{\sigma}^{L}\prod_{t=1}^{L}\left(\left\lVert\Delta\Theta_{\Psi_{t}^{(l)}}\right\rVert_{F}+\left\lVert\Theta_{\Psi_{t}^{(l)}}\right\rVert_{F}\right),
